## Post 43 by Khyron Overmind on 2008-09-20T13:14:36Z



Onboard the Avenger, the Tactical Officer reported.

**"Sir, we are receiving information from the Satellite Deep Scanning Probes (SDSP). The composition of the enemy fleet is as follows:**

**3 Super-class Star Destroyers**

**8 Imperial-class Star Destroyers**

**16 Victory-class Star Destroyers**

**12 Carrack-class Star Cruisers**

**8 Heavy Dreadnaughts**

**2 Interdictor-class Star Destroyers**

**Sensors also report a strong compliment of fighters and bombers and ships capable of housing ground assault troops for planetary invasion."**

Rear Admiral Sarriak considered the information for a couple of seconds. The MoD Fleet alone had enough Protector Class Battleships in order to outmatch the enemy Start Destroyers. Considering that the Battleships sported superior weaponry, shields and better hull, they were covered. The support ships would also outgun the rest of the ships. The only problem could be the 3 SSDs. It would take a lot of firepower to counter them. The good thing was that when the IR capital ships were designed, in addition to better and more weaponry, they had larger fighter compliments. In addition to that, the Coruscant Sector Fleet was also in system, so they definitely had enough advantage to take out the incoming Fleet. That did not mean that it would be easy. In fact, it would be a nasty battle and with tons of casualties on both sides.

**"Rely that information to General Cognatus and Admiral Firecam. Also, launch all fighters but order them to remain in pattern. As soon as they come within range, hail them."**

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Back in the Palace Situation Room, Grand Admiral OVERMIND started to issue instructions to divide the people they had into teams. He was waiting to see if Colonel Shan would be able to join them in order to split the forces into four groups: Bodost, Bremen, Shan and himself.

## Post 46 by Malia Jae'Nor Hollis on 2008-09-20T16:58:38Z



Malia halted in her tracks, spun around on her heels, and tried to keep the annoyance from showing in her eyes as her gaze landed back on Revan Centurion, who was engaged in conversation with another officer. **"Sir, Incomming transmission from Coruscant, you'll want to see this."** Locke immediately turned his attention away from minor plans he had been working on to the large tactical system map that was now displayed before him. There was definately quite a capable hostile fleet that had arrived in the system, and he knew that the local forces would not be able to handle things on their own. **"Inform our forces that we will be departing for Coruscant immediately. Prepare for engagement as soon as we exit hyperspace, have our fighters ready for combat deployment."** He paused for a moment, **"Patch me through to our fleet."** His bridge coms officer went to work, securing a connection and then gave the Admiral a thumbs up. **"Attention Republic forces, this is Admiral Firecam. Hostile forces are assaulting the Palace, all ships prepare to make the jump to hyperspace. Data on the enemy as well as jump coordinates are being forewarded as I speak. This is what we train for people, we will not fail the Royal Family in this time of need."**

He returned to his seat, pulling out his comlink and opening a channel to Lieutenant Kasra. **"Lieutenant, make sure our squads are ready to launch. You have a green light as soon as we exit hyperspace. Time is going to be critical here, so I'm leaving the preparations to you. Data on known enemy forces will be forewarded to you shortly."** Looking down at his datapad sighed to himself, he had kept much of the information about the attack secret, but he knew that it would not last for long. Opening up his contact list, he sent a quick message to his most trusted Army officer onboard asking how the investigation into his royal guards as going and again making sure they are being watched. Leaning back in his chair, he watched the stars streak by as the ship entered hyperspace.

I could set the fighters onto their bombers to prevent them from damaging any of our ships. The Highwind and the MoD fleet should be able to take on the enemy fleet but we will all sustain large casualties. hmmmm... Ok I am going to send the bombers after the SSD's gun emplacements and have the fighters pick off the other fighters"* Talak stood up and began the short walk to the briefing room, all the while thinking different battle plans.

As he reached the briefing room he was surprised to see that all squadrons were suited up and there before he was. He took his place at the front of the room beside a large holoscreen. he placed his datapad into the receptical on the podium, typed in a few commands and the image changed to Coruscant. **"Alright boys, we have a little intel as to what we are walking into but not much so listen up. From what our SDSP's are telling us, we have a good sized fleet on our hands. 3 Super-class Star Destroyers, 8 Imperial-class Star Destroyers, 16 Victory-class Star Destroyers, 12 Carrack-class Star Cruisers, 8 Heavy Dreadnaughts, 2 Interdictor-class Star Destroyers, and a whole masquerade of fighters and bombers to be exact. Now, we are going to be entering the system here"** Talak pointed to a spot that was behind and to the left of a few red dots that indicated the enemy fleet **"Now what I would have you do is the bombers will target the SSD's gun emplacements so that their firepower is out of the way. While they are doing this, fighters, your going to focus all your fire on the bombers and fighters on the enemy fleet. This will distract the fighters from picking off our bombers. I warn you now, there are goings to be numerous casualties on both sides, but YOU, bombers, can help us reduce those on our side if you take those guns out. After the guns are out on the SSD's, I want 3 fighters to every 6 bombers and I want the bombers to try and take out the Heavy Dreadnaunghts all together. Any extra fighters, do your best to eliminate those transports. Am I understood? Good, now get to your ships and get ready."** *"And good luck, for all it's worth"* Thought Talak as they began to file out.

Talak retrieved his datapad and began to walk to the bridge, all the while sending a message to Admiral Firecam to tell him that his squadrons were ready to fight for the Royal Family.

## Post 52 by Revan Centurion on 2008-10-02T14:05:19Z



Revan nodded his head slightly to the General. **"Yeah...I can sense as much too..."** Turning his head back towards the palace, Revan allowed his feelings to expand outwards. Even as it eveloped the area around the palace, he could distinctly sense a number of individuals. People he'd dealt with in the past, primarily. It was at least comforting to know that they were still alive.

Withdrawing his presence before it could be used against him, Revan turned back to the General and pulled out his datapad. **"Once inside, I'm going to head for Sanctuary."** Knowledge of the highly-guarded meeting location was limited largely to the High Council. The ability to enter the facility...well...it was hard to say. It had not been used at all since the High Prince's departure some years ago. He wondered if the guards even remained...if they did...he wondered if they'd even recognize who still had access. *Too many things changed...it's going to take time to bring all the various elements of the Imperial Republic back up to speed.*

Quickly, Revan dialed in the multi-character code to disable the Palace Shield. Once the code was accepted, he provided a voice sample. Those two steps confirmed, the system showed that it was ready for the final test. Holding the datapad up to his eyes, Revan allowed the retina scan to be conducted. When all was said and done, the various fail-safes deactivated. In the next few moments, confirmation of shield deactivation was received.

Nodding to General Cognatus, Revan smirked slightly. **"Good hunting General. You know how to reach me if you require anything further."** Revan was referring to the force, naturally. Silently and swiftly, Revan withdrew the black hilt of his lightsaber and set off in the direction of the Palace with the aid of the force. Small tendrils of force lightning crackled around his knuckles as his danger senses began to rise steadily. *Sean...*

## Post 54 by James Stratus on 2008-10-02T19:48:20Z



**Sanctuary**

The High Prince led the way toward the portal. As he approached the droids looked in both directions, then looked to him. He began walking forward, and pressed his code cylinder into the wall authentication system, and keyed in a code. A green light came on and the door hissed open. A dim light showed a long path-way to another chamber. They could see red lights along the walls of all directions. As the door slid shut behind the party, more lights came on, and the red lights were revealed as the eyes of hundreds of Sentinel droids. None of them were as large as the ones that guarded the entrances to the facility, but were still quite impressive and large. The High Prince had only seen specs on the droids and had only interacted with the prototype that was used to program hard-wired recognition of himself, so that all droids would recognize and respond to his commands, regardless of programming. Since the departure of the genious and highly valued intelligence director Jenov Raveshaw, the droids were not in active service as he was the only one beside the High Prince that had the codes and ability to control them....theoretically. The access had never been given to Layha, though it had been the original plan to do so. They had been forgotten even by the High Prince and so he had not done so. However, there was a certain Deputy Director of Intelligence that had worked closely with the former director, that had been his successor, though not presently. He too would likely have the access codes to override and control the droids, though not against the commands given by the Prince. The Prince made note of that fact and continued walking down to a highly secured chamber that would give him access to the Intelligence-Defense Network Grids. This would be useful in finding out exactly what was going on. No transmissions could be made from this control center, without an override, which he, under the circumstances, was not about to give, as he did not want this facility compromised. It would, however, give him insight, as to what was going on. The army of sentinels stood at attention as if recieving the arrival of a highly esteemed guest, or presenting themselves for inspection. Though the High Prince knew perfectly well what they were really doing. Even now their programming was assessing every member of his present party and were calculating threat levels, how to remove those threats, and how to protect specified targets. All sorts of computations and calculations were going on through each individual droid's intelligence circuits, but also being processed by 'leader' droids and a central droid command system. Even *he* would hate to ever be on the opposite end in a conflict with a group of these, much less a small army.

As they came closer he paused. He tried to get a feel for who and what was out there and what was going on, but the facility was force-cloaked by the presence of an impossibly large number of ysalamiri. They approached the door, and came to a halt.

## Post 56 by William Benson on 2008-10-09T05:33:47Z



**Royal Palace on Coruscant - 10th Level - Ministry of Finance Main Offices**

***(Before and During the Palace Communications Jamming)***

William Benson had been in the middle of an important meeting with some attending via holovid, one of those included Executor Treyson who was on Hapes on a diplomatic mission. They were discussing the interest rates currently set on government bank accounts, when he was suddenly cut off by the sound of static. Many screens that were on different displays all went blank. He looked around the room, as did the other officials present. He keyed the comm for his secretay. Static. The Minister stood up, and the others followed suit in respect for him standing. He opened the door and his secretary stood up, waiting on the other side. **"What is going on? I was in a transmission with the Executor. Call communications and get our link re-established."**

The secretary nodded and then had a surprised looked on her face. **"Minister, it appears that all palace communications are being jammed, including interpalace communications. We can't get out, or within."**

**"Send someone down to the communications centre and see if they can find out what is going on."**

**"Yes sir."**

Soon after the messenger left, Klaxxons sounded throughout the entire palace. The Minister looked up at the door. **"We must secure this area. Initiate lockdown procedures."** He said, though a bit too late. The secretary had managed to start the automated lockdown system for the Ministry floor just after a few of the armed guards with their weapons aimed at the Minister and his secretary arrived.

The Minister had his hands up as well as his staff.

**"If you cooperate with us, no one will be harmed."**

The Minister kept his cool. It was the first time someone had tried to "rob the bank" so to speak in his career. **"If you are who you are dressed to be, you should already have access to what you seek. However, I must inform you with all seriousness that I do not have the ability to disburse funds while the palace communications are jammed - so is the funds disbursement system, 2) I cannot disburse funds while under duress, and 3) I can only disburse funds to persons previously authorized from budgets previously approved by the Throne or the High Council, and by the heads of the department of the budget in question. You should know that I cannot simply disburse funds without your dept head's approval (when communications are restored) and only a limited amount is disburseable without budget approval."** He looked at them cooly and calm. They argued back and forth, until they were at a stalemate, standing for a long period of time until the Minister finally sat down and the others did the same. The area was in lockdown and only security could unlock it now and call an all clear, which could only happen when the communications were restored. The Minister could not override it, though he did have access to emergency escape passageways that lead to the Security Offices, though he was not sure that was a good idea either.

![williambenson-sig.jpg](http://images.imperialrepublic.org/sigs/williambenson-sig.jpg)

Four Sentinels stood in circle around a battle command center, identical to the one in the Palace Situation Room. There were like command centers throughout all military and similar ones, though this one was very advanced, an exact replica of the one that undoubtedly Admiral OVERMIND was probably standing over. The High Prince observed with curiosity the four Sentinel droids standing over it like tactical officers and military commanders. These droids had a gold stripe lining around their torsos. They were clearly communicating and pointing at the tactical map in a fast computer language. When they recognized the Prince they stood up to their full height and stood at attention.

The High Prince, acting as if all was normal, spoke. **"As you may know my own Royal Guard unit has turned against us save this one here,"** he indicated Lieutenant Tav, **"and we lost communications in the Palace."**

The droid leader made as if to nod. **"Yes My Lord. The specs of the fleet appeared on the screen. The ships names were locked and classified. That was odd, since this was the Ubiqtorate Sanctuary. Unless...The High Prince suddenly became fully alert. **"Initiate Emergency Clearance Alpha Blue. Voice authorization sufficient for emergency clearance."**

***"Emergency clearance activated."*** The ships identifcation names began to display on the screen, as well as a few more ships that had not been on the screen before.

## Post 58 by Revan Centurion on 2008-10-16T22:17:26Z



Silence was all that surrounded Revan as he dropped stealthily to the marble floor below. The palace was eerily silent, but the force seemed to be...overly active. If it had been his younger, more inexperienced days, Revan likely would have trouble making sense of it all. At present, he had accessed the palace from one of its top levels. Climbing to the pinnacle of the gigantic edifice had taken a small amount of time, but the gain was worthwhile. Revan was well aware that the most secure areas of the Palace were in the sub-levels. Access to the Temple of Hope could be found from one of the sub-levels. Access to the Ubiqtorate Sanctuary was also there. *A place I've not visited in...forever...* Still, that was only one rather small part of the issue. The High Prince was fully capable of defending himself. He did not need Revan's direct assistance to stay alive. No longer an official member of the Imperial Republic's Administration, Revan certainly had no obligation to the government for much of anything. Sure, he maintained a few advisory roles at the request of the High Prince, but his concern centered more on maintaining balance within the force. Without that balance...all was inevitably lost.

As he progressed along the dark corridor, Revan's dark overcoat glided lightly over the top of the polished floor. Within a matter of moments, Revan had reached one of the shafts of the many lifts. Motioning with one of his fingers, Revan opened the heavy doors with the force. Holding on to the edge of the molding, Revan looked down. The lift was at the very ground floor. It was one of only a handful of lifts that serviced the levels of the palace belonging largely to the Throne's direct IRIS support staff and the Director's office. Sighing slightly, Revan stepped over the edge and into the shaft. Dropping rapidly, Revan held his arms out to the side...more so because of the force of air rushing up underneath him than anything else.

Nearing the ground level, Revan oriented his left palm downward. In rapid succession, he sent a flurry of powerful pushes with the force towards the lift on the bottom. The severe force of the energy slamming into the lift pushed aside the metal in both the top and bottom with relative ease. Several more powerful bursts ripped into the very flooring of the lift shaft. Managing the force to control the speed of his descent in its final stages, Revan landed softly in a corridor of the Palace's first sub-level amidst a number of confused guards preparing to redeploy to another area of the palace. In an instant, the silver blade of Revan's lightsaber activated. Raising his hand, Revan lifted the two closest guards immediately into the air before snapping their necks. Before their bodies had even hit the ground, three more were attacking Revan from behind him. Spinning low, Revan swept out and up with his lightsaber at the left-most royal guard, splitting his body in two. A number of blaster shots lanced out at Revan, but he managed to quickly deflect the bolts with quick action from his lightsaber. Pushing the two guards backwards, Revan hurled his lightsaber directly into the heart of one prior to unleashing a powerful force lightning attack on the other. Revan could smell the man's skin and armor burning under the intense heat and force of the attack before he released the man - still alive.

Casually, Revan walked to the severely injured guard. **"Who are you working for?"**

Silence.

Revan raised his voice this time. **"Who are you working for!?"**

The guard's only response was to produce knife. With whatever last remaining amount of energy the man had, he lashed out towards Revan. Reacting deftly, Revan grabbed the man's arm with his left hand and called his lightsaber from the dead guard's chest back into his right hand. Smirking, he shook his head. **"Your lack of cooperation is unfortunate."** Twirling around the hilt of his saber with his right hand, Revan severed the man's head from his shoulders. Releasing the limp arm, Revan deactivated the silver blade of his weapon and took a few steps back. His breathing was a bit heavier, but he managed to calm his nerves. There were still far too many questions to be answered. What was worse, Revan had a feeling it was all a very small part of something much, much bigger.

**Lord Revan Centurion**

*Praetorian*, Imperial Republic

*Lord Steward of Naboo*

*Shadow Jedi Master*

## Post 59 by James Stratus on 2008-10-17T02:43:32Z



**Orbit of Coruscant**

Three naval officers stood at the tactical sensor display on the bridge of the *Prince's Pride,* viewing the scene with dismay and concern on all of their faces. Reports had come in that the size of the fleet had increased by 40% of its original size. The Admiral watched in dismay as the names of the ships began to appear on his screen. Undoubtedly the leaders of the other two fleets in orbit were doing the same, though Admiral Tinkel had a bit more information than they did. The Royal Fleet alone could identify these vessels because they were, in fact, Imperial Republic-flagged vessels under the command of one of the High Prince's own members of the Throne. With communications lost to the Palace, Vice Admiral Tinkel, as commander of the Royal Fleet, would have battle command, until a member of the Throne, or perhaps even Throne Regent, was appointed.

The time was near, the fleet had arrived almost in orbit and close enough in range to do some damage. The planetary shield was still down...*Come on, Ground Troops, do your stuff!* the Admiral thought.

**"Shall I hail them, sir?"** Captain Rooney, captain of the *Prince's Pride* asked. Standing next to him was Commander Scott Mcfayden, the Royal Fleet Wing Commander.

The Admiral transmitted a secure message to the three official fleets, the Coruscant Defense Fleet, the Defense Command Fleet, and the Royal Fleet. **"All ships, prepare for combat. Battle stations. Standby for my orders. I am declaring battle command until further notice. Whatever happens, we must protect Coruscant. Nobody goes in without ground command authorization."**

The admiral sighed. Now they would have to wait for the Red Fleet's move. He didn't like this at all. He did not have the authority to authorize an attack on any group unless attacked first, and certainly not against another Imperial Republic fleet. The authority behind that fleet might also prove to be a problem.

**"Admiral, the Red Fleet is launching troop transport shuttles. It appears they are heading for the surface,"** the captain announced.

**"Barry, we can't let them go to the surface. But we can't open fire without authorization unless they fire first. This is not a good situation."** *Too bad we don't have any Throne members or military leaders to consult with besides ourselves,* he thought. **"They are leaving me with no choice. We can't tell the rest of the fleet who they are, as that is classified, and it could compromise all sorts of secrets and cause all sorts of chaos. We'll just have to let them think they are simply an enemy...for now..."** He turned to the communications officer. **"Open a channel to the Red Fleet."**

## Post 67 by Revan Centurion on 2008-10-21T01:36:25Z



The silence continued to be eerie. Revan was used to the Palace more or less being a constant bustle of activity. Discreetly, he inched along the corridors until he reached his destination. It was another lift, but he intended to actually utilize it in the normal sense this time. Just to get the door to open, Revan was forced to place his palm against a scanner, enter a four-character code, and submit to a retinal scan. Once the doors had opened, Revan stepped inside. As the lift began its descent, Revan shut his eyes and allowed his feelings to wash over the area in and around the Palace. There were so many people in motion...so much anger, concern, confusion. Really, the multitude of feelings could have very well been overwhelming to even the most seasoned of force users.

As the lift came to a halt on the lowest level, Revan opened his eyes. The now constant green shade of his eyes caused them to glow in the perpetual darkness of the corridors. Just beyond the lift, Revan could see that there was a medium-sized speederbike. If he remembered correctly from his days within Imperial Republic Intelligence, it was the only real way to have any hope of reaching his final destination in a reasonable amount of time. While there was not presently any guards down this far, Revan knew it would only be a matter of time before they managed to make their way down to this level. Still, Revan was rather positive that it would be almost impossible for them to go where he was going. *Only one way to be sure...*

Quickly, Revan took a seat on the speederbike and shot off into the dark abyss before him. In order to keep from killing himself, Revan relied largely on the force to guide his course. The corridors all looked the same, and it was nearly impossible to find the right ones unless you already knew where you were going. The entire time he was driving, Revan continued to have this nagging feeling that something terrible was still yet to come. More to the point, he had a feeling he knew one of the people likely behind it, but he sincerely hoped that he was wrong. *\*Layha... I hope you know what you are doing...\** He didn't know if she was behind it, part of it, anything. There were some things that they just didn't say to each other. Very often...that practice was for the better. Life was complicated enough without their views on the force colliding into each other full strength.

As Revan was approaching a very solid wall in the distance, he wracked his brain trying to recall if that was the correct entrance. He could not sense anything on the other side of the wall, so he suspected that it was the correct one. The only certainty at this point was that...he would know for certain in three, two... Slamming on the airbrakes, Revan lept high into the air as droids in the room immediately opened fire on his transport. Landing softly on the ground with his lightsaber crossed in front of his body, Revan crouched low.

"Identify yourself or be destroyed."

Revan forced his nerves to calm, but he kept his lightsaber activated. **"Director Revan Centurion..."** Sanctuary hadn't been used or activated since...well...way before he was ever made Executor. As such, he didn't have access codes in that incarnation.

"Director Centurion. Voice sample confirmed. Please place your palm on the reader."

Revan slid his palm into the large receptacle and waited for the various security procedures to be completed. Once everything was done, a door at the far end opened. Deactivating his lightsaber, Revan casually made his way for the main entrance to Sanctuary. Even as he stepped inside, Revan was instantly reminded of how immensely secure the facility was. Off in the distance, Revan could see that someone was inside the control chamber. It could only really be one person, but the place was surrounded by Ysalamiri. Utilizing the force to confirm his suspicions wouldn't be very useful. Revan approached the chamber cautiously, pausing only to have his retina scanned once more.

Entering the control chamber, Revan was greeted on his approached to the Battle Command Center by a number of blaster shots fired from the Sentinels assigned to protect it. Raising his right hand, Revan captured the energy in a large blue ball before allowing it to course throughout the entirety of his body in the form of force lightning. The Sentinels immediately assumed a more neutral posture as Revan spoke and approached Jim's group of people. **"Director Revan Centurion, Access Code 1WSC."** One of the Sentinels nodded slightly in his direction as Revan approached Jim, his expression neutral if not slightly irritated. After all, the day he'd warned everyone of had finally come. **"Are you all alright?"** There would be time for honors and ceremonies later, but he did afford the High Prince a polite, half-bow.

**Lord Revan Centurion**

*Praetorian*, Imperial Republic

*Lord Steward of Naboo*

*Shadow Jedi Master*

Most doors had a killswitch that would open the door without the log indicating it. And this door, thankfully, was wired the same. She crossed the two wires that sparked, causing the large barrier to be swept up into the wall above and give them access. The benefit to this would be a few extra moments of not being hunted, if it came to that.

Mali and the members of the Alpha Company ran down the hall in formation, for the most part, with Mali leading the pack with the map. She took the third left, and then the first right that lead to an old staircase that spiraled downwards under this section of the massive palace. Running as fast as she could, and not really stopping, she followed the indicator on her datapad screen almost carelessly until she got the gut feeling. The one that always preceeded bad ...

"STOP!" She yelled, just as a large blast hit her square in the chest, making her fall backwards into the first line of soldiers. The blast burned clear through her uniform coat, but was easily deflected by her Mandalorian armor. While she caught her breath, soldiers pushed past her taking a defense to ward off whatever was blasting at them. Mali calmed her breathing, and looked at the map on her screen. They couldn't afford this distraction. Then she saw it ... The vents. She looked to the right, and sure enough, the entrance was there.

"I need two men to cover me." she whispered, and two men/officers near the end of the line nodded. She pointed to the vent, and kicked the cover off. It would be large enough for them to go one at a time. She crawled in and to the left, centering herself over the room they would need to access. She heard the soldiers behind her, and did not stop to wait for them. They would have to be capable enough on their own.

She found a drop that would allow her to access the control room. She took the drop as quietly as she could, and peered through the vent cover. She saw no one. She pushed the cover off and pointed her riffle blaster, doing a sweep. No sign of intellegence beyond the control pannel. She ran out, and to the pannel the instructions described. She found the right screen, and entered the code. An alarm klaxon beeped, and a strange metallic voice said, "Planetary Shields Activated."

Just then, the message from the General came in. "Lieutenant Hollis I need a report have you been successful?"

The other soldiers were in the room now, securing the door and disableing the security that had originally shot at them.

"Roger General, Planetary Shields Activated." She knew she may have sounded winded, but she tried her best to hide it. Just then, she heard a large explosion. "GENERAL?"

**Edited October 22, 2008 by Malia Jae'Nor Hollis**

## Post 73 by Locke Firecam on 2008-10-22T05:40:37Z



The Admiral watched the stars streak by and slowly return to normal as the *Highwind* exited hyperspace. **"Status report."** The order came out as second nature, as reports indicated that the ship was now combat ready. **"Sir, incomming transmission, shall I patch it through?"** The coms officer seemed suprised, but he knew that being hailed on exit was to be expected, **"Yes, make it happen."** Moments later the image of a Republic fleet commander appeared before him.

**"Admiral Firecam, good to see you, and just in time. We need some decisions, fast, if you have the authority to do so, otherwise we as fleet CO's are prepared to act independently in this situation, but I fear that will be a mistake. I am transmitting you everything that has occurred in the last 15 minutes of critical importance. I standby for your orders, Sir."**

The Admiral replied with a slight nod, **"Understood, thank you for your time I will assume command at this time."** Turning to his coms officer again he quickly picked out a number of targets on his scan report. **"Open a channel with our friendly forces."** The young officer again worked quickly, **"Ready to go Sir, you're live."** He paused for a moment, collecting his thoughts. **"Loyal forces of the Imperial Republic, it is with great sadness that I inform you of the great crimes of treason that have taken place here today. Due to these unfortunate turn of events, I am hereby declaring throne regeancy in this emergency. All loyal forces are to rally under my flag at this time."**

Cutting the link for a second, he opened up his datapad and prepared to send out a global coded message, *Emergency situation, assuming command to combat internal threat, unable to verify status of critical targets. Transmit code: Firecam Delta Four Omega.* Immediately his datapad was flooded with access to previously unknown areas. Resisting the urge to poke around in this newfound wealth of information, he quickly scanned the library for files relating to the Royal Guard and the Red Fleet. He would have to quickly sort through them once orders were issued.

Taking took a deep breath, thinking carefully about his next statement. **"I'd like to thank our hidden enemy for being so gracious as to announce his presence....Fleet Admiral Ordenai and Red Fleet, I am here to pass judgement on you and your men. Their ships are to be added to all loyal forces systems as hostile targets in addition to those already flagged. You all have been trained to handle these situations, do not let a single transport reach the surface. Assume formation around the** ***Highwind*** **and stand our ground between the enemy and Coruscant. Destroy any small craft trying to break through, but do not engage capital ships unless fired on. If they fire a shot, weapons are free and give them the swift deaths that traitors deserve."**

He took a step back, knowing that there would be much doubt about what he had just done. If he was wrong, it was very likely that his career would be over, and his own life would probably end shortly after. Opening up a private channel with the forces of the Red Fleet, he felt a sly smile cross his face. **"Admiral Ordenai, I'm sure by now you know what the situation is. Your authority here is over, your ships are to power down immedately and stand by to be boarded or be deemed hostile to the Republic and dealt with accordingly. The choice is yours."** Letting the comlink close for good, he turned to face his bridge crew, who looked at him with amazement over all that had just happened. The silence was broken by one of the more senior bridge officers reporting in that many ships had begun to assume formation and were dispatching craft to deal with the transports.

**"Excellent,"** he said, turning around and drawing his sword from his side and pointing the blade toward the window, **"Commence the counter attack, and launch our fighter squadrons to help intercept. This rebellion ends here."**

**Lord Admiral Locke Firecam**

Deputy Minister of Defense - Operations

Imperial Republic High Councilor

## Post 81 by James Stratus on 2008-10-24T01:03:49Z



**Royal Fleet in Orbit**

**"Return fire! All ships target the** ***Avada Kedavra.*****"** He turned to Captain Rooney and motioned for him to come over. ***"*****Barry, I want you to put the crew on alert to be prepared to abandon ship on a moment's notice."**

The Captain frowned at him. **"Do you really think they'll use the superlaser?"**

**"Without a doubt, Barry. I think they will fire that thing the first chance they get, and I have no doubt that we will be their very first target. This vessel has been the flagship of the entire Imperial Republic, until Throne Regency was declared. If they had any military sense they would attack the Highwind first, but knowing who is behind these attacks, and even with a military perspective this is the most powerful vessel present in this battle."**

**"What about the failsafe system?"** the captain asked.

The admiral frowned and spoke softly. **"*****If*** **it is still in place, which I will grant is very probably, the fact that they are here with that size of force leads me to believe they have found a way around it, or perhaps have the codes necessary to fire the way it was designed. I have a gut feeling that is precisely the case."**

**"Shouldn't we advise Admiral Firecam?"**

The admiral nodded. **"Indeed, and with haste we must.****Comm, get me a a priority-one transmission to Admiral Firecam,** ***now*****."** He waited until the connection was made.

**"My lord, be advised that the Red Fleet flagship is the** ***Avada Kedavra*****, and it is equiped with a superlaser**. **We believe this vessel will be the first target. I recommend, Sir, that we concentrate fire on the superlaser weapon. It is highly shielded and has heavy armor. We won't be able to prevent its first attack, but it does take time to recharge, though not nearly as much time as it took the Death Star of the old Imperial forces. This is a much more advanced weapon. If we can prevent it from firing twice, by taking it out, then we have a chance. At the current distance, we have no chance of stopping its first attack, not even at ramming speed----**

**"Sir! Energy on the Red fleet flagship has shifted. High power is detected in the front of the ship sir, like nothing I've seen before."**

The admiral nodded. **"My Lord, they are powering up their weapon. I estimate we have approximately 2 minutes before it will be operational."** He ended the transmission. *If he wishes to respond, it will automatically reconnect. Right now I have a ship to prepare.* He turned again in the direction of Captain Barry Rooney. **"Captain Rooney**," he said aloud. **"Commence maximum fire power on the central cortex of the superlaser,** ***and****,"* he added, this time loud enough for all to hear, **"prepare to** ***abandon*** **ship. That's an order!"**

It was a very difficult decision he would soon have to make, unless of course a higher authority made it for him. Ultimately it was the captain who had the final say in the matter, unless of course a commanding flag officer was present. Though Admiral Firecam had the authority to relieve either of them of command or change the orders. In the entire history of the Imperial Republic the *Prince's Pride* had never been evacuated or abandoned once since its commission. It had been in many battles, had won many victories. His responsibility was to the Throne, but also to all the many lives that were aboard. **"*****Highwind,*** **we standby for orders."** He turned to his officers. "**We'll see what the chief says. Though he is not the Supreme Ruler, we recognize him as having the same authority in his absence, and we** ***will*** **act accordingly."**

## Post 82 by Khyron Overmind on 2008-10-24T02:49:36Z



Rear Admiral Sarriak had received the feeds regarding the present situation unfolding in Coruscant's orbit: the Avada Kedavra was about to fire a superlaser weapon within 2 minutes. Without hesitation, he gave his orders.

**"All gunners, fire upon the Avada Kedavra and neutralize their superweapon. All fighters and bombers, launch their attacks at once."**

He assessed the situation and then decided to give an additional instruction.

**"Order all ships in Alpha and Beta Lines to get into Theta 7 formation and advance at flank speed towards the Avada Kedavra and position themselves between the Prince's Pride and the Avada Kedavra. All ships, re-direct all power to forward shields. And keep firing!!"**

Theta 7 formation meant that all ships in Alpha and Beta Lines would fly in close formation, overlapping their shields, thus multiplying their defense power against any weapon. All onboard the Avenger and within the other ships in the Line knew that it meant their certain death but nobody objected the order. All had been trained to fight and they all knew that at some point, they would meet their end. Saving the Prince's Pride could be seen as a futile gesture, considering that thousands of lives would be terminated. But that the Prince's Pride represented was even more important than them. They were aware that 8 Battleships, 8 Cruisers and 8 Frigates overlapping their shields would not stop a superlaser, but they only needed to absorb or deflect enough of that energy so that the Prince's Pride could resist the first shot. With all the might of the remaining ships in the MoD, Coruscant and Royal Fleets, plus the reinforcement brought by Admiral Firecam, the Avada Kedavra would never fire a second shot.

Inside his head, Rear Admiral Sarriak was making furious calculations. Over 70 Defender Class Battleships were in orbit above Coruscant and a similar number of Defender Class Battleships and other types of ships were converging their fire upon the superlaser, plus all the fighters and bombers. If he was not mistaken, they would put the superweapon out of service before it was ready to be fired.

Something definately was not right here. **"Sir, incomming priority transmission for you, shall I patch it through?"** He replied with a quick nod, and the image of the ranking officer onboard the *Prince's Pride*.

*"My lord, be advised that the Red Fleet flagship is the Avada Kedavra, and it is equiped with a superlaser. We believe this vessel will be the first target. I recommend, Sir, that we concentrate fire on the superlaser weapon. It is highly shielded and has heavy armor. We won't be able to prevent its first attack, but it does take time to recharge, though not nearly as much time as it took the Death Star of the old Imperial forces. This is a much more advanced weapon. If we can prevent it from firing twice, by taking it out, then we have a chance. At the current distance, we have no chance of stopping its first attack, not even at ramming speed----they are powering up their weapon. I estimate we have approximately 2 minutes before it will be operational.*

The link was cut and Locke stood motionless as the shock of what he had just been told sunk in. This was a true crisis, as that weapon would be able to fire at least once, this he was almost certain of. Something had to be done, and quickly. **"Open up a secure channel with the** ***Pride*** **immediately."** The coms officer worked quickly, and gave him a thumbs up, indicating that he was now live.

**"Admiral, thank you for the information. We don't have much time, but believe me when I say that the** ***Pride*** **will not be sunk, not on my watch. It is a symbol of our Republic and one that must be protected. Have faith in our men, and we will make sure you all live through this day. Divert power to your engines and form up behind the screen we will create for you."**

He had seen data readouts dealing with the capacity of the so called super laser. It was quite powerful, but even though it could destroy a ship in one shot, it was unable to pierce through and hit a second target, as the energy was spread over the primary target. It also had to obey the laws of physics, meaning that it would have to travel in a line. With a sigh the Admiral shook his head. He was going to lose a ship to that damned weapon, that he was sure of, but it would not be the flagship of the Republic.

**"Attention all loyal forces, this is Admiral Firecam. I am forewarding the data readout on the enemy flagship** ***Avada Kedavra*****. Destruction of this target has been given highest priority as it is capable of firing a super laser."** He paused for a moment, uploading a file from his datapad to be sent to the fleet. **"We will be adopting a new formation, centered on creating a screen for the** ***Prince's Pride*****, we have two minutes tops to shield her from the secret weapon of the enemy. Keep it tight and make sure that the** ***Kedavra*** **is unable to get a firing solution of the** ***Pride*****. Once the weapon is confirmed to be disabled or destroyed the** ***Pride*** **will be safe to rejoin the battle and we will surge foreward to victory. Protect our flagship at all costs!"**

He knew very well that the damage the loss of that ship would cause to the morale of the men would be far greater then the loss of any other warship currently on the field of battle, and he would take the hit himself before he would let that happen.

**Lord Admiral Locke Firecam**

Deputy Minister of Defense - Operations

Imperial Republic High Councilor

## Post 84 by Ashlee Gourdine on 2008-10-24T06:37:44Z



Ashlee had been seperated from Marc and Nicole en route to the bunker. She had encountered quite a lot of problems along the way. She didn't feel them anywhere near...it felt very dark. Something was very wrong. Out of nowhere weapons from all directions were pointed her way. She raised her lightsaber but was too late, they hit her with a stun blast, and she was out...

Darkness...

She woke up feeling pain in her side. *Where am I?* She looked around and found herself surrounded by royal guards. *Good, I'm safe.* Then the thought struck her. It had been the royal guards who had attacked her in her room while she was sleeping - and it had been them that had ambushed her.

She heard voices. **"Good, she's awake**. **Let's get her to the bunker. The Grand Vizier wants her moved right away."**

*Grand Vizier*. Hah! Some nerve. Did they actually think they were operating under legitimate authority? Lord Kennedy was, indeed, as far as she knew, the Grand Vizier of the Imperial Republic, a member of the Throne. He was not known to the public, and had done a lot for Jim, even saved his life on many occasions. He was also, the most powerful *Dark Lord of the Sith* she had ever known. She had never trusted him. She and everyone had always felt uneasy in his presence, and avoided him. Jim had been the only one immune to the effects Kennedy seemed to have on anyone. She had never sensed any uneasyness in Jim. Even Layha she had known was nervous around him...until recently. She had seemed to develop the same strength and immunity to that presence that Jim had while he was gone. Though she secretly wondered if she had anything to do with all of this, herself. She had acted rather strange immediately preceeding and after Jim's return to the Throne. It was obvious Lord Kennedy was behind all of this.

**"Alright, lets move her!"**

*Over my dead body*, she thought. *I won't let Kennedy take me alive. It will only be used as leverage against Jim, and in the end it will end up worse for both of us. It won't be hard for him to kill me, but I can't let him take me.*

She cooperated with the "guards" along part of the way, just long enough to get her bearings and figure out which one of them had her lightsaber. As soon as they turned the corner, she reached for the lightsaber through the *Force* and managed to send a powerful blow to her binders, shooting them off and away. She took a few hits in on of her arms and legs. They were aiming to kill this time, which helped her deflect the shots. One of them yelled something as if he remembered they were supposed to take her alive, and she was able to jump out of the way and kill the attacker. She got to the last guard there and sent the ligthsaber running through him just as he fired a stun shot, and both of them were knocked out...her unconcious once again...and him...dead, the lightsaber still ignited on the ground a few feet away.

Darkness...

**Princess Ashlee Stratus-Gourdine**

4th Princess of the Royal House Stratus

Member of the Royal Supreme Court

Director - Stratus Industries

*Shadow Jedi Knight*

## Post 85 by Revan Centurion on 2008-10-24T18:21:38Z



James did have several valid points. The most important being the fact that Sanctuary very much was a void in the force. It afforded them certain defensive advantages, but it also blinded them from anything that could be happening within the force *outside* of Sanctuary. Still - Jim was right. He most certainly did need to stop leading the fight so often. He appointed people to various duties for a reason. Until recently that had included Revan. Though, his resignation hadn't gone without a bit of resistance from present members of the Imperial Republic Throne. Sighing lightly, Revan responded to the High Prince's question. **"To be honest. I don't see that we have much of a choice. We're going to have to wait it out...at least until the palace can be completely secured. I'm assuming communications aren't being jammed from** ***within*** **the palace. Surely there are a variety of override codes in place for that very reason."** Revan was not exactly positive what had or had not been changed since the short time since his resignation. He knew such fail-safes existed when he was in charge of security.

**"Which of course leads to the question...where is all this being coordinated from?"** It was possible the nearly forgotten Red Fleet could be controlling the situation, but it would not explain how all these procedures had been initiated and underway before the Red Fleet ever even arrived. Then a thought struck him... **"...who has an active UAC from the throne right now?"** Revan's should have been deactivated. Still...seeing as how his access to a lot of other sensitive information and systems had not yet been...he wasn't sure. Which also meant that Tavria likely had not been given one yet...and that Layha's was still active. *Dammit Layha...* Revan wasn't condemning her, but there were simply far too much evidence pointing in her direction. He had faith she wasn't behind it, but he preferred to stay ahead of the curve. Both to find out who was really behind it...and protect Layha if she did have anything to do with it in the first place. He already knew that she knew more than she was saying. That was enough to piss him off, and she was certainly going to hear it from him.

I know they won't fire on Coruscant, not with Lord Kennedy on the surface. From the information I have gathered at this facility, it would appear that Sean's been here for approximately two weeks before the attack, and it appears he has been to both Nexus Core and Alpha Point. If Admiral OVERMIND manages to figure out** ***how*** **to get there, he may be able to get in. If anyone else tries, I'm afraid it will not fair well for them. There is still two troop transports full of ground troops and assault mechs headed our way, a strong hostile fleet in orbit, and some of the most highly trained and deadliest special forces to eliminate within the Palace and tunnel system. ## Post 88 by Revan Centurion on 2008-10-26T12:52:35Z



Revan was silent for a number of moments as Jim responded to his question. Thankfully, the news was far better than he had expected it to be. In truth, the various issues with the present IR Government could not all be laid at Jim's feet. He had been gone for a very long time. The problem was that his 'Regent' claimed she didn't want to change anything he had done - even if it was for the better. Obviously that got thrown out the window when she decided to walk the path of the Sith. Now the government would be forced to recover from the mistakes of many - including Revan's. Perhaps that was the real reason he was standing here. Could he truthfully consider himself retired? *Yeah...I am.*

Nodding his head, Revan glanced over at the large tactical display. He didn't have a career history of being any military strategist, but he certainly understood it well enough. Though if given the choice, he did tend to leave such things to the experts. His own specialty was direct, covert action and various methods of psychological and information warfare. He was...a manipulator...to put it bluntly. **"Well the way I see it... Admiral Firecam will be able to handle the situation in Orbit. If what you say about the Avada Kedavara not firing on Coruscant is true, then we have a bit of an advantage. Just depends on how gutsy the Admiral is willing to be with his forces."** Revan paused briefly before stealing a glance at the other individuals in the room. **"We need to find out where Lieutenant Colonel Shan is..."**

Making his way over to a nearby console, Revan utilized his own access code to pull up the most recent security information on the palace. Given the present lockdown, the information was likely to be several hours old already. **"Hmm. She should still be here then."** Turning back to Jim and, in effect, Koru, Revan continued. **"We need to start securing high value locations here on the surface. Admiral Firecam can do his thing in orbit...and he** ***is*** **helping to buy us time."** Revan of course had no idea that Firecam was presently acting as Throne Regent, but it mattered little to him at just that moment. **"If we can actually locate Colonel Shan, her help could be very, very beneficial."** Pausing for a moment, Revan wondered who in the room was aware of Kaidlen's affinity with the force. Revan's was fairly well known amongst most government officials at this point - same as Jim. Kaidlen, however, still managed to keep her own abilities discreet. *Definitely a good thing.* **"She could lead a strike team to either Alpha Point or Nexus Core. I can lead one to whichever location she does not select. It's the best option we have right now, really. Admiral OVERMIND and his forces have already begun securing the palace...we should hopefully have that in hand eventually, but it's a huge facility. It's going to require a lot of time...more time than this battle is likely to even last. That's why we really, really need to get communications restored. It's crippling us to a very large degree."**

## Post 100 by James Stratus on 2008-11-06T21:30:00Z



**Sanctuary**

**"It would appear that the Princess Ashlee is in need of our help. It also appears that we are the only ones in position or capable of helping her, or even aware of her plight. The time to head for Alpha Point is near. I have held it off as long as I possibly can. Perhaps some will join us on the way, though it appears the primary objective is currently to get to Nexus Core."** He then whispered to himeslf. **"I wondered how long it would take them to discover its existance and even harder, location. My compliments to Beta Team."** The High Prince looked at his team. It wasn't much to look at. There was himself, Miss Lance, Koru Tav, and Revan Centurion. He looked at the monitor showing the status and readiness of the battle droids.

One of the commander droids spoke. **"My Lord, Alpha Regiment is at full battle readiness and perpared to accompany you at your command. It is recommended that Beta Commander and Beta Regiment remain here along with the Centurions to remain at their posts and protect Sanctuary."**

The High Prince nodded and considered. He would very much like to have Beta Regiment *and* the *Centurion*-class super-battle droids to accompany him to Alpha Point, but he knew it was vital for them to remain here. He looked over at Koru. He was ready and itching to go out and do something, anything. He smiled at the man's youth and resolve. Glancing at the battle droid leaders, he noted no expressions whatsoever then the constant sober and slightly diginified look they were capable of displaying. Looking over at Miss Lance, he saw in her a fiery beauty. He felt the need for her to stay close to him no matter what. Truthfully he wanted her to stay here in Sanctuary protected by the guards, but he knew she would never go for it. They had been through too much together, and she was pretty good in a fight herself. He would need every bit of help he could muster. Finally, he looked over and faced Revan Centurion. Revan had served underneath him in the Imperial Republic in a vast number of important roles and positions, and had been a great asset to the Prince and the Imperial Republic as a whole many times over. Yet he was a mystery. He valued Revan in high esteem, and would even venture to consider him a long-time friend of his, but he wondered where he was in this whole matter. He fully trusted him presently, and immensely hoped that Revan would do nothing to discredit that trust. His confusion was in regards to another old lifelong friend, who had changed immensely over the years, especially during his absence. He wondered, indeed, where *she* stood in this whole ordeal. He would hopefully find out, be in the end, he might not.

**"Revan, we're kind of in a tight situation. We're running out of options. We must get to Ashlee and rescue her from enemy hands. If you are willing, I would ask you to be primarily responsible for getting her to safety. I will undoubtedly end up in some conflicts of my own with the Dark Lord. What say you?"**

## Post 106 by James Stratus on 2008-11-09T03:08:13Z



**Orbit of Coruscant**

Aboard the Prince's Pride, klaxxan alarms sounded shortly before the firing of the Superlaser. As the beams began to reach their focal point, a grave voice from the command deck shouted **"*****Clear the bridge! Clear the command deck! All hands, prepare to abandon ship!"*** Vice Admiral Tinkel looked in the direction the order had come from. He found it odd that it was coming from someone other than himself. Before he could open his mouth, Captain Rooney looked him straight in the eye and with a very stern and authoritative voice said **"Get to your shuttle, NOW. That's an ORDER, Admiral. I am the commander of this vessel and I am ordering you to evacuate to safety, as per Military Code S153 Section 2. MOVE!"** Admiral Tinkel wanted so badly to challenge the captain and hesitate, but he had a gut feeling to go along with it, so he did as quickly as he could. The Admiral moved from a swift stride to an incredible run, with a small trail of fleet security officers right behind him. The bridge which had been very orderly though extremely busy with the battle, suddenly became utter chaos. Officers and bridge crew were scrambling to the escape pods that went from the bridge, while many others were boarding emergency shuttles and transport vessels preparing to launch. All but two....that is. The Admiral noticed as he looked back that Captain Rooney, after barking at everyone in sight to get out, had sat down in the captain's chair, with two other officers next to him. He didn't have time to do anything about it, though he wanted to. *Blast it, Barry! What are you thinking?!! Get off the damn ship while you still can!* He managed to get aboard his shuttle with the shields up and the engine on as he heard a loud blast. The shuttle ejected from the large flagship and the admiral watched painfully as two noble ship captains put their ships between the superlaser and the *Prince's Pride*, causing severe damage to their vessels, and many lives. The impact on the two battlecruisers had caused a series of explosive reactions resulting in heavy damage to the front of the *Prince's Pride,* wiping out the bridge, the command deck, the forward weapons and shields, and a good portion of the front of the ship. He watched as escape pods and shuttles shot out from all areas of the vessel, though he knew a sizeable crew would stay aboard to try and salvage the ship and save it from total loss. If they hurried, they would be able to secure 2/3 of the ship and keep it from self-imploding. The automated systems would have kicked in by now starting that process along. As he watched the bridge collapse, his thoughts turned to Barry....*No! No No No! I should have made him get on that shuttle with me!* He could only hope that the captain had managed to get to an escape pod or somehow survived....but he knew Barry, as he had served with him for many years. He was a captain that would go down with his ship, although it wasn't entirely going down. He switched on the comm and opened a channel to the nearest ships. **"Imperial Republic vessels, this is Vice Admiral Tinkel. We have some battleships and the Prince's Pride that have suffered heavy damage and are in need of rescue runs. Escape pods and shuttles that need to be retrieved right away!"** He turned to his pilot. **"Find the nearest battleship and lets get this thing aboard. Let them know I will be joining them at the bridge."**

Koru walked silently up to the weapons stash with a mischievious glint in his eyes. As he surveyed the stash, his eyes passed over the rifles and quickly found their way to the blaster pistols. He immediately chose his poison: a single DL-44 heavy blaster pistol and a small single strap bag. Once he pocketed several cartridges for his standard blaster pistol, he grabbed the DL-44 holstered it onto the other side of his belt and pocketed several DL-44 cartridges. He quickly unholstered the heavy blaster pistol and made sure it worked (no he didn't fire it; though the idea came to mind). He slipped in a fresh cartridge into the heavy blaster pistol and reholstered it leaving the safety off. Lastly, he grabbed four thermal detonators. He was about to turn away when he spotted two lonely det packs. Koru grimaced at whether or not to take it. He then shrugged his shoulders, picked it up and placed them in his bag along with the thermal detonators. He took off his quiver, slung the bag over his right shoulder, then reslung his quiver. Now satisfied he turned to see Dana looking at him with an eye raised.

**What?** Koru asked as he gave a mock smile of innocence. **You never know if you might need something extra** Koru said as turned to follow the prince.

Onboard the Avenger, Rear Admiral Sarriak jumped from his position in the command chair to check the damage reports of the ship. The Avenger had sustained several Turbolaser hits that managed to get through the shield gaps when they flickered trying to absorv all the hits. The reports indicated that even though several hits were received by the superstructure, all main systems were up and running. Therefore, the Avenger was going to stay in the battle for a while longer.

The next thing was to check how the rest of the ships of the combined Fleets were doing. The scanners indicated that the MOD Fleet had lost a couple of battleships and several others were damaged as a result of all the firing and explosions. The Prince's Pride was the one that took the most severe punishment. He did not need to check the scanners for that, since looking out through the bridge windows, it was clear that nodoby had survived in the bridge, as it was non-existant. However, there was always the secondary bridge.

**"Comm, contact the Prince's Pride and find out who is now in command"** he said with a tone of regret in his voice.

**"Also, send some shuttles to search for survivors. Locating Admiral Tinkel is a priority."**

And at that moment, the transmission from the Admiral was heard.

**"Imperial Republic vessels, this is Vice Admiral Tinkel. We have some battleships and the Prince's Pride that have suffered heavy damage and are in need of rescue runs. Escape pods and shuttles that need to be retrieved right away!"**

Then he turned to the Weapons officer and the Fighter Coordinator.

**"All weapons, fire at the thirs Eclipse Super Star Destroyer and keep ourselves to the starboard of the Aveda Kedavra, so they can not fire at us. All fighters must assist with the attack. Throw them everything we got."**

## Post 111 by Dene Vye Cognatus on 2008-11-10T02:47:40Z



(edited)

Dene walks back outside since somehow he forgot that he was inside....

General Cognatus looked through his binoculars at the approaching Heavy Assault Mechs and others, as well as the considerable enemy stormtrooper regiment, all with red stripes across the shoulders. *At least they helped us identify them from our own,* he thought. Standing next to him on a makeshift command platform near the Palace, was Colonel Guy Haymon, the Coruscant Legion CO. **"General, the enemy assault mechs approaching. Request permission to activate our own Assault Mechs and heavy assault vehicles and bring them into action. Sir it is most critical that we put them to use."**

General Cognatus thought for a moment. It would definitely be a cause for much chaos and fear among the citizens of Coruscant and likely be a source for many innocent deaths and destruction of many buildings, even coming from friendly firepower, to bring those out. But he knew he had no choice. He nodded. **"Permission granted, Colonel Haymon. Get them out here, and fast. And Colonel...sound an alert throughout this grid, all civilians are to evacuate to safety and get as far away from the Palace and shield generator areas immediately."** The Colonel saluted and walked away quickly to issue orders. It was then that Dene remembered another military operation taking place on the surface, near the shield generators. He had recieved reports of enemy forces headed there. He walked over to the communications officer and was able to establish communication with the unit there, if only briefly. **"Lieutenant Hollis, I'm sure you're probably about to be as busy as we are with the enemy forces. You are probably already aware by now you have an enemy unit headed your way. Do whatever is necessary to prevent them from getting to the shield generator** ***at all costs*****. You are hereby authorized to make use of whatever assets the military or the government has in that area to your advantage, and take out whatever stores and supplies you need. I suggest you set out to work immediately. Good luck, and may the Force be with you."** Dene turned back as Colonel Haymon arrived. "**Colonel, get our troops and mechs ready, and see what you can do about getting some portable shield generators running to put a small shield up around our men to protect us from an assault from the distance until they are in close proximity for us to take them...oh...and bring the heavy cannons online. Inform them to report when ready, they will fire on my mark."** *I wonder how the battle is going inside the palace, and also in orbit.*

Dene started to prepare himself for the fight. Suddenly he felt a rush of pain and fear in large degrees. Many people had just died. He wasn't sure where, but he had a feeling it was related to the space battle. *That* was not a good sign.

## Post 112 by James Stratus on 2008-11-10T03:36:13Z



**Coruscant Orbit**

Aboard the command shuttle *Little Prince*, Admiral Tinkel was impressed with its speed an manueverability. It had been the shuttle used strictly by the High Prince, and the Admiral wanted to get that valuable shuttle to safety, and knew it would provide him some protection as well. He had managed to get as many officers and crewmembers that were in sight that he could fit on this shuttle aboard, so it was a bit crowded. The original pilot was flying her, and he was seated in the the seat where the Prince himself usually sat. *I would never do this under different circumstances*, he thought. He wanted to save every bit of the Prince's important ships as he could, and he felt a failure for not being able to prevent what had taken place. At least it wasn't completely destroyed. It was very much salvagable, and repairable, though it would take a few weeks and a good space station with the capabilities - if they could manage to get it there. In orbit of Coruscant were facilities capable of repairing it enough for it to limp to the Kuat sector where it could be worked on, though never before had it been taken there for repair. The origin of the *Prince's Pride* was the very space station that built the *Avada Kedavara,* none other than *the* Centerpoint Station. Now that too, would be a place of battle, and not too long from now he expected, once the High Prince was back in action and the military leadership was enlightened on the subject. It would very soon become declassified to many military leaders he expected. He recieved the transmission from the *Avenger*. He couldn't remember the last time he had been aboard that vessel. It was indeed an excellent vessel, though he expected it was probably in some rough shape at this particular moment, due to the circumstances. He opened a channel to the ship, responding to the admiral in command of the Minister of Defense's fleet. He had had no intention to take command of that fleet. Rear Admiral Sarriack was himself a very capable fleet commander. Ideally he would join with a capital warship from his own fleet, but at the moment they were all engaged closely with the enemy ships and the *Avenger* was the closest, having been pulled back to the rear as it had suffered some damages itself. It would be useful for the time being, to coordinate the Royal Fleet attacks from there. He was no longer Battle Commander, as when Admiral Firecam arrived, he yielded command immediately to him, especially upon him declaring Throne Regency. This was serious business indeed. He was always weary of anyone taking on such authority of the entire Imperial Republic other than the already established Throne. He had worked with Admiral Firecam on occasion and trusted him to be of sound mind, and knew he was loyal to the Prince. He did not expect to see any problems upon the Prince's return. **"Admiral Sarriack, I thank you for the offer. Normally I would probably decline due to the need for me to be with my own fleet, but since that appears to not be possible at the moment, I graciously accept the offer. We will come aboard immediately. I request permission to join you at the bridge upon my arrival. I have quite a few officers from the bridge of the** ***Prince's Pride*** **and crew members. Feel free to put them to good use until this thing is over. They will return with me to the Royal Fleet when this is all over, but for now, they are at your disposal."**

A few reserve fighters had launched from the *Prince's Pride* and were escorting the *Little Prince*. They arrived aboard the *Avenger*. Waiting in the docking bay were a few medical personnel and droids, as well as a small security compliment and one of the *Avenger*'s bridge officers, Lieutenant Dameon. He saluted Admiral Tinkel and the others as they unboarded. **"Welcome aboard the** ***Avenger*****, Admiral Tinkel. Admiral Sarriack apologizes for not being able to greet you personally, but I am sure you understand under the circumstances. He would be honored if you would join him on the bridge. Your bridge officers are welcome to join us as well, I am sure their skills will be helpful."**

The admiral nodded and walked quickly, following the lieutenant with a few of his officers trailing behind, as well as a couple security officers from the *Avenger*. The rest of the shuttle's crew were being treated by the medical team and given some rations. As the Admiral came to the bridge command deck, officers and crew snapped to attention. **"Admiral on deck!"** shouted a bridge officer. Admiral Sarriack offered him a quick salute, and then went back to the hustle and bustle of the battle. The officers from the *Prince's Pride* that had managed to come with the shuttle, took places on the bridge to assist the *Avenger's* crew in the areas they were used to assisting on the *Pride*. Vice Admiral Tinkel joined Rear Admiral Sarriack on the bridge.

**"Might I offer a suggestion, Admiral?"** Admiral Sarriack nodded and Admiral Tinkel continued. **"My sources tell me that there is a huge weak spot on the far end of the engine exhaust ports on the** ***Kedavara*****. I believe if we concentrated our fire at this target** (he pointed to a particular spot) **we may be able to do some considerable damage rendering the enemy ship completely unstable. It's just one ship, but it will give us a big advantage."**

Admiral Sarriack thought for a moment. **"Very well, Admiral Tinkel, we shall do that. Would you care to give the orders, Admiral?"**

Admiral Tinkel smiled and nodded. **"Thank you Admiral. This vessel is still under your command, but I will gladly give the orders."** He turned around. **"Weapons control, cease fire, repeat, cease fire! Helm, bring us about to the rear of that eclipse as quickly as possible, full shields. Target the exhaust port in grid A-1130. All weapons fire on my command."**

When the *Avenger* was finally in position, the captain reported readiness. All eyes turned to Vice Admiral Tinkel. They watched him. He looked very determined. **"Fire."** The command was direct, authoritative, and cold, and at his command, all remaining weapons aboard the *Avenger* opened fire, and the *Avada Kedavara* took fire, and began to self-implode with explosions occuring in all aspects of the ship. **"Admiral Sarriack, get us out of here,** ***now!*****"**

**"Yes sir!"** he said quickly, as he took command of the *Avenger* once again. **"Helm, reverse engines, full power! Get us back to our fleet where we can join the battle from a safer distance!"**

## Post 113 by Dene Vye Cognatus on 2008-11-10T18:39:55Z



(edited)

**"All cannons report ready General Cognatus. We were only able to get one of the portable generators online and the power is running low. Its only a matter of time before it goes out sir."**

The general nodded. **"Very well. All canons, fire at will. Let's do what kind of damage we can while there's still a chance. And if you have any missile launchers, now is the time to deploy, Colonel Haymon."**

**"Yes sir."**

*It's a good thing we're on Coruscant, where we have a heavy stash of weapons and assault vehicles. We have not always been so lucky.Not to mention the shield generators. Coruscant was one of the few facilities that had tons of them, though apparently only one was working...how ironic. When they actually needed them....No. It was no coincidence. It was deliberate*.

Dene checked his belt to make sure that he had his prized weapon at his side, as well as all kinds of other weapons that would prove useful. The ground rumbled as the heavy canons fired, and as the the Imperial AT-ST and AT-AT walkers returned fire, vibrating heavily against the shields.

**"The shield won't hold much longer, General. It's about to collapse."**

Dene nodded. **"Take cover! All troops and vehicles move into position. We have to stop them from hitting the palace. Let's go!"** he shouted. Both he and Colonel Haymon began to take their positions on the ground with the rest of them. Colonel Haymon climbed into one of their own equivilant of the AT-ATs, though much more powerful. Dene was supposed to get into one himself, but he decided much against military preference and the urges of his officers to remain directly on the ground in his own stormtrooper armor arrayed with blue stripes, like the rest of the Coruscant unit. It was truly a marker against the red-striped enemy. When the enemy was in plain sight, it was time to move.

Dene raised his hand, and swung it forward. **"Attack! All weapons, fire!"** He then begun with the rest of the group firing at the enemy.

## Post 114 by Revan Centurion on 2008-11-10T19:22:25Z



Ordinarily, Revan would have relished the ability to let his presence flood the force once again, but such was not the case today. Even as they emerged from the confines of the secret enclave, Revan remained somewhat withdrawn from the force. It had certainly been one of the more...interesting things to learn when he was younger. The concept of allowing yourself to become no bigger than the tiny particles that made up the life force...at least in terms of non-visual perception...had been somewhat difficult for him to wrap his head around. Forcing himself to adapt, he later realized how useful the skill would be when he started working full time for the Imperial Republic. *Now I'm doing it to keep myself and others alive...* For the time being, Revan didn't exactly see moving through the network of tunnels beneath the palace as a great idea. Who knew how many more Royal Guards or other forces had penetrated the cavernous levels of the Palace and were simply lying in wait? The surface was a bit more obvious, but they had a good deal more control over their surroundings. That is to say...they had a lot more options should something go wrong.

*And it always does...*

**Lord Revan Centurion**

*Praetorian*, Imperial Republic

*Lord Steward of Naboo*

*Shadow Jedi Master*
