## Post 115 by Malia Jae'Nor Hollis on 2008-11-11T02:51:53Z



Mali was beginning to feel her third-or was it fourth-wind of adrenaline. She had organized with the men on the other side of the arsenal outside, and as they had neared each other, they discovered the weaponry had been from a stand-alone security system involving motion-detector gunnery stations mounted in the hall ceiling. The guns were mounted with a 360 degree shooting radius. Without the code words, they were to shoot anything coming or going.

From the point of disarming them, Mali was able to reconnect a manual security option, keeping the room under the control of a person instead of the programmed presets. She ordered two men to stay inside and lock themselves in. It was not ideal, but until they could figure out what was going on, she ordered them to respond only to her, Cognatus, and the top of the chain of command.

She and the rest of the troops set out to find the General, going the way they came. They encountered little resistance, since many had fled to shelter. Mali didn't like it. Something wasn't right. Not far from the room, there was a shaking of the floors and walls. Mali ducked and crouched close to the wall as the trembling stopped. Mali rose, the fear of what was coming next turning her cheeks red with anticipation and worry.

They moved faster, Mali taking the lead, checking out corners as quickly as she could and moving onwards to the large door. But they did not make it far. Blasts lit the hallways casting an eerie red glow on the walls, and pinging sound of deadly energy prickling their hearing. Mali ducked, just as her comm device sputtered to life.

***“Lieutenant Hollis, I'm sure you're probably about to be as busy as we are with the enemy forces. You are probably already aware by now you have an enemy unit headed your way. Do whatever is necessary to prevent them from getting to the shield generator at all costs. You are hereby authorized to make use of whatever assets the military or the government has in that area to your advantage, and take out whatever stores and supplies you need. I suggest you set out to work immediately. Good luck, and may the Force be with you."***

*Great, now he tells me …*  she thought as she dropped her barely active energy cartridges to her long range blasting riffle onto the floor and loaded two new ones. She crouched down, followed immediately by the other soldiers, and began to inch forward, trying to get close before the enemy found them.

**Lieutenant, we have a situation …** one of the soldiers whispered behind her. **You aren’t very familiar with the layout here, but there is a second shield generator. For the planet, I mean.**

The new information did not ease Mali one bit. **Where?**

**It’s in another building, near the Prince’s quarters. Closer to the Throne Room.**

**Take two men and secure it. Same deal. I don’t care if they try to blast you out, keep it secure unless the orders come from the Army Command or the Throne itself.**

**Aye, Lieutenant.**

While the smaller group separated and went out a back way, Mali and the others began to advance. They neared the sound of the blasts, and discovered that they came from right outside the entrance they had used. The door was open, leaving the hallway exposed. Mali noticed something odd. There was a Palace Guard standing nearby, who seemed less than concerned that a group of Troopers and two walkers were within a few feet of a Palace entrance. The guard must either be a traitor, a fool, or in trouble. And she saw only one positive outcome for any of the three titles.

After arranging the remaining soldiers to be half on one side of the corridor’s intersection, half on the other, Mali stepped into the corridor’s entrance, took aim, and shot. The guard fell to the ground, and the Troopers gave her their full attention as they lifted their weapons.

Mali darted back to one side of the hallway, flattening herself against the wall and motioning for her men to move back. She pulled two thermal detonators from her pouch, and waited till the entrance filled with storm troopers before activating them, throwing them, and running like hell. The explosion was very effective, leaving less than ten troopers mobile outside with the two walkers. The soldiers came running towards her, shooting the Troopers on the ground a few times for good measure, and took stance for when the Troopers would round the corner.

![malia-sig.jpg](https://images.eotir.com/sigs/malia-sig.jpg)

**The willing, Destiny guides them; the unwilling, Destiny drags them** (Seneca: Roman philosopher, mid-1st century AD).

SIDIOUS:I have waited a long time for this moment my little green friend.Huch huch hu at last the jedi are no more.

## Post 116 by Koru Tav on 2008-11-11T04:41:39Z



**I hate this.** Koru mumbled to himself as he looked back behind him past the rear portion of the group. They had been traveling about 5 to 7 minutes through the tunnels from Sanctuary. But the problem with the word "tunnels" is that it is grammatically incorrect. The correct word is tunnel. **One single long tunnel with absolutely no cover whatsoever**. A giant killzone just waiting to happen. Koru didn't have any doubt that his "comrades" in the Royal Guard had found the entrance to the tunnels or tunnel. As the group pressed on Koru extended his awareness via his eyes, ears, and smell. The tunnel was illuminated by the occasional dim light. There were few nooks and crannies on the wall that offered no cover if there was a firefight. There was the occasion scurrying of the small animal or the drip of water. There was no wind or breeze which caused a musty and old smell to somewhat permeate the tunnel. *This is just going* ***too*** *well.* Koru thought the same thing as Revan.

As the group continued on one of the "crannies" they passed caught the Lt's eye. It was deeper than the others, just enough for one man to go back several feet and hide themselves completely from the light. He squinted and made up his mind that his head was playing tricks on him as he thought he saw a miniscule glint of metal. Koru kept walking and rubbed his eyes to wet them. For some reason his mind went back to the time when him and his dad were playing hide and seek: combat style. Someone always was slightly injured but the game tuned your senses very well. He remembered one of the times that he lost. His dad had snuck in a nook or cranny of a midsize tree. Koru remembered that he had seen the glint of metal but shrugged it off: that mistake gave Koru a good cut on his shoulder making him lose the round. Koru frowned, stopped silently, and slowly made his way back to the "cranny" without a sound. He focused on his hearing and was sure that he heard something lightly against the wall. He quietly nochted on arrow and pulled the string back. He saw a forehead of a helmet appear slowly and aimed right at it. When the whole face appeared, there was the miniscule twang of the string letting go of the arrow. The pop the arrow hitting the head was followed by the thud of the body hitting the wall from the speed the of the arrow hitting his body.

Koru made his way over the corpse and pulled it fully out into the open, relieved it of its comm, pocketed a detonator, and confiscated his arrow. He swished it around in one of th puddles to clean it and made his way quickly and quietly back to the group while checking when the comms messages. Thankfully the man hadn't given his postion away to his superiors as the communiction would have been intecerpted by Sanctuary. The comm would also be a good tool to listen in on enemy chatter and learn their movements. *This little walk was going too well indeed.* Koru thought as he kept his senses on full alert for danger.

![korutav-sig.jpg](http://images.imperialrepublic.org/sigs/korutav-sig.jpg)

## Post 122 by Dene Vye Cognatus on 2008-11-12T19:41:57Z



Dene watched as the enemy advanced and as the palace began to take damage to its outer structure. The Prince was not going to be very happy about this. He looked around at the grazed homes and buildings. It was not a pretty sight. It might be different if it was not directly in front of the Palace view, but then again maybe not. The High Prince was particular about this sort of stuff, always defending the citizens and wanting to protect them, but Dene was doing his best, in his defense, and in theirs, actually (![:P](https://nexus.eotir.com/uploads/emoticons/default_tongue.png)). He had a bad feeling suddenly and looked up as he saw a quick flash and then the ground rumbled harder as a blast from an AT-ST hit real close to him, knocking him unconcious with several injuries.

Colonel Haymon watched as the blast hit General Cognatus. **"The General has been hit! Take him back to the command post and get him medical attention immediately!"** The Colonel then resumed command of the ground combat. Overall, the battle was leaning to their advantage. The enemy was extremely outnumbered. Though Colonel Haymon wondered if that would still be the case if the other troop transports had been succesful in getting through the shield and surviving the attack from the defending fleet in orbit to prevent them from their destination. It would be a much worse situation indeed. Still...the general down...that was bad news. Hopefully they would get him back on his feet soon. They needed his leadership. The battle would not last much longer. The enemy was running out of men. Haymon wondered if Lt. Hollis was having any success. She was facing a larger regiment than her own team, but she had a lot of advantages on her side that if put to use would prove easily victorious...he hoped.

Dene put himself into a healing trance immediately before he went unconcious. The next thing he remembered was opening his eyes and seeing a medical droid looking down at him. **"What happened to me?"** Dene asked.

**"A laser blast from one of those walkers hit only a few feet from you I am told. You're going to be just fine, Sir. I'll need you to remain laying down here for a few more moments while I finish treating you. You've got some bad burns on your skin, but your armor protected you for the most part. Nothing broken."** He gave Dene a quick shot of some various ingredients to help fight infections and pain.

## Post 124 by Corran Halcyon on 2008-11-15T18:30:02Z



**Somewhere in Chiss space**

**"Uhhh.... were am I. What happend.... The Triton!... Oh my head!..."** Commander Halcyon slipped back into unconscience. Another few days went by.

Corran's eyes snap back open. **"What. Where am I.... Hello.. anyone here?"**

Corran looked around the room. He was in a medbay, but it didn't look like any IR medbay. It was dark but he could make out tall figures moving around the room. **"Hey, where am I?"** Corran yelled.

**"Your safe."** Came a voice on the other side of the bed.

Corran turned and came face to face with two glowing red eyes. **"Sithspit. Lev? What in the world are you doing here? How did you find me?"**

**"Corran calm down. I'm going to tell you everything. The Triton was destoryed. I was in the area on a job, and herd about the battle. We started to come out here to see what was going on. We picked up the radio chatter and herd that you were the Commander of the Triton. Next thing I knew there was an explosion on your ship. We waited until the battle was over to see if you were ok. The rest of the IR fleet was doing there regular post-battle searches as well. We used the cloaking shield to move around undetected. We found some bodies floating around were your ship was. We beamed them aboard and started to look for you. I found you a few minutes later. You had a faint vital sign. We moved you into the medbay and started to revive you. And now here you are."** Lev said with a smile.

Corran just sat there quitely. **"Hmm...its funny how roles change."** looking up at the Chiss sitting next to him.

Lev started to chuckle.

**"So where are we?"**

**" We are back in Chiss space. The IR is still under attack."**

**"Well then I need to get back and help."**

**"Corran, you need to rest for a little while longer, and you dont have a ship, remember."**

**"Oh, yea. Well how many ships do have under your command now?"**

**"Two, Corran."**

**" Well we can still help, can't we?"**

**"Yes we can, but right now you need to rest. We will talk more about in the morning."**

**"Oh, ok, we wait till morning."**

**"The same old Corran,"** Lev said with a smile.

Next Morning

Corran got up and went to the wall locker to get dressed. **"Go figure, a Chiss Navy uniform."**

Corran got dressed and proceeded to the bridge to join Lev.

**"How do you feel"** asked Lev. **"Oh I feel great. So where we headed?"**

**"Well I figured we would start at Courascant and see how the battle is there."**

**"Sounds good to me."** said Corran.

**"Oh, by the way you will be commanding the Ghoststalker. Both of our ships have the cloaking shield, and can only communcate with each other while in stealth mode without giving away our position."**

**"Ok got ya"** Corran was transfered to the Ghoststalker, a modified Carrak cruiser.

**Onbord the Ghoststalker**

Commander Halcyon went to his room to get ready for the trip. He decided to check his personal messages for any information to what was going on in the IR.

**Urgent Message For Commander Halcyon**

**From: Prince Stratus**

**"This looks interesting."** Corran opened the message.

**Commander Halcyon,**

**If you are still alive I need you to go to Hapes in covert. Rescue the Executor of the Imperial Republic who is in danger and attempts on her life are being made. Bring her back to Coruscant. Carry it out ASAP.**

**Stratus**

**"Well I guess we arn't going to Coruscant."** Corran keyed his personal comlink with Lev. **"Hey, plans changed we are going to Hapes, to rescue the Executor of the Imperial Republic."**

**"We doing what, are you crazy Corran?"**

**"Lev I still work for the IR, and this mission came from the Prince."**

**"Oh ok fine we go."**

Commander Halcyon went to the bridge.

**"Navagation, get us to Hapes."**

**"Aye Sir."**

Corran and Lev were soon on their way to Hapes.

## Post 127 by Revan Centurion on 2008-11-18T23:22:17Z



Revan crouched low as he approached one of the secondary entrances to the Alpha Point bunker. The approach to the facility itself had actually gone off relatively well. With Revan's extensive knowledge of most Imperial Republic security facilities, he knew exactly where to go and when in order to avoid confrontation. While he did not know exactly where Ashlee was being held, he assumed she would likely be in one of the secure rooms near the shuttle escape access. If that was the case, then it wouldn't be a good idea to start tripping alarms until that plan of extraction could be neutralized. Satisfied that the door was unguarded from the outside, Revan quickly placed his palm on the reader and entered the alphanumeric code.

As the heavy doors slid open, Revan quickly slipped through the entrance. Darkness was all that met him on the other side. Allowing his feelings to expand over the area of the compound, Revan gripped the black hilt of his saber lightly. *I know there is a terminal around here somewhere...* With the help of the force, it didn't take Revan too long to locate what he was looking for. However, his mind was decidedly more attuned to the fact that...he'd encountered no other personnel as of yet. Activating the terminal, Revan quickly used the variety of clearance codes that he still knew to deactivate most of the secondary security systems. Chief amongst them were the motion sensors he knew to be arrayed throughout the facility. Next, he locked down the shuttle escape exit and encrypted it with a new password. *Thank goodness for this place having very few redundant systems...* The lockdown itself would only hold for so long, but it was the best that Revan could do for just this moment. Now...he just needed a distraction to allow him greater success at getting to Ashlee. Plus...he really did not need to run into Kennedy prematurely. It was almost like using Jim and his forces as bait... *Greater good Revan. Greater good...*

A few simple keystrokes, and the primary defenses to the main entrance were deactivated. Revan went as far as to overload all of the electrical arrays that produced the various shields protecting areas of the bunker. The effect would neutralize any guards near these arrays and thusly...anybody too close to any of the entrances. *\*Jim. You should be good. I'm going to find Ashlee, I believe she's near the rear of the facility. I've locked down the shuttle bay for now, but it won't hold for long...\**

**Lord Revan Centurion**

## Post 128 by James Stratus on 2008-11-19T04:39:32Z



They had arrived at the blast doors for Alpha Point. As Jim reached out through the *Force*, he quieted his mind and waited. He heard a tiny *click*, and the doors began to open. Revan had perfect timing in opening the blast doors. He heard shouting and bustling of the enemy as they began to prepare for what they must have known was coming. He held up his hand signaling his men not to fire until he gave the signal. A lightsaber popped into his hand from nowhere visible and ignited. Blaster fire poured upon them from the open doors. He raised his hand and motioned forward, giving the signal. The droid troops immediately began pouring in their own blaster fire. He was quite impressed with their targeting efficiency. He grinned at the look on the faces of the guards as the deadly battle droids filed in. They clearly, had not expected this. These droids were nothing like the old battle droids of the Trade Federation. Their design was quite different as well, and they were highly intelligent, one reason they were kept locked up away from the public. They could not be replicated but only manufactured and produced within the secret facility of Sanctuary itself. They had built in program and hard-wire protections against that. Their armor was very strong. It deflected most blast fire. The High Prince moved swiftly, cutting down all those in his path, deflecting bolts, and sending waves of destructive force in all directions. Lord Kennedy was getting closer, he could feel his presence, eminating strong in the *Force*. His own presence was currently but a shadow, by his own will. He continued to slash down the enemy guards. He turned to Koru and the droid commander. **"Once Lord Kennedy is in sight, and you will know him instantly, stay out of the way, and get out of the immediate area and find other enemies to destroy, and guard the way from anyone else coming. Do NOT fire on Lord Kennedy, or you will die instantly. I must fight him alone."**

The droid commander acknowledged and the droids immediately began to scatter and take formation marching in another direction, while some stayed to prevent others from entering what had now become a battle arena...for Lord Kennedy had arrived.

**"Ah, my Lord,"** Kennedy said sarcastically, **"I have been expecting you. You are late, mi'lord."**

**"Why, Sean? Why did you do this? Did your Sith traditions finally take over and consume you? After all these years, after all we have been through together...you turn against me now? If this was to be then why not long before?"**

The Dark Lord of the Sith laughed. **"You have put too much faith and trust in your friends once again, my lord. That has always been your greatest weakness."**

**"Perhaps,"** the High Prince responded curtly, **"but as always** ***you*** **are overconfident in your own strategies. Even now, your fleet is being destroyed, and its only a matter of time until your palace regiment is also fallen."**

**"You are mistaken, fallen one. It is your government that is in shambles. Now the people will be looking for reform, now I will give it to them. My apprentice lies in wait to trap and destroy your special team headed for the Nexus Core. They will not survive. They will be no match for him, just as you...are no match for me..."**

Through the Force, the High Prince established his true presence, in full spectrum. A very powerful aura fell around him from all over, so strong that any who were remotely Force sensitive throughout the entire system would feel his presence, and those not sensitive to the Force who were closeby would see the aura. **"I am much more powerful than you can possibly imagine, Lord Kennedy, thanks to you."**

It came very suddenly, as Kennedy's outstretched hands aimed in the direction of the High Prince, with blue lightning bolts pouring from the Dark Lord's fingertips.......................

....................only to be met by purple lightning eminating from the fingertips of the High Prince, which never before had been witnessed or seen from the High Prince by anyone save the one person to whom the lightning was striking. Initially it overpowered the Dark Lord as it took him by surprise, and some lightning managed to hit him. He tried stronger, but the Prince was even stronger. The Dark Lord's eyes had become pure evil, whereas the High Prince's eyes had become as blue fire, as in righteous justice or defense. There was nothing evil about the the manner in which the Prince conducted himself, or the feelings or emotions eminating from him. He and the entire government had been betrayed by the Dark Lord. Many of his men and women had been murdered. The High Prince was a Shadow Jedi Grand Master. He did not regard the Force in the same manner that the Sith and Light Jedi did, though there were many similarities. He had achieved a unique balance in the Force, and did not lean in either direction. Anyone who were to see him at this moment would not dare to cross him, save the Dark Lord of the Sith and his followers. He sensed the "apprentice" Kennedy had spoken of. He was no apprentice, it was very similar to the presence of Igor Murashkin, himself a strong Sith Lord, who Layha had killed. It was a *clone*. So Lord Kennedy *had indeed* found the hidden cloning facilities aboard the secret space station. It did not surprise the High Prince much, but it did concern him greatly. That meant a lot of things. He would have to go there with all haste when the conflict on Coruscant was resolved. As he focused his attention back on the Sith Lord, he withdrew his own lightning, and began to absorb that of the Dark Lord as well, creating a deflection orb, which he launched at Lord Kennedy.

**"Reform may be needed indeed, Lord Kennedy, but not the reform you have in mind."** The Dark Lord moved just as the blast hit him, only taking a small part of it. His red lightsaber whipping out from his cloak and igniting. In response, the High Prince's own lightsaber, just like before, appeared into his hand from what appeared to be thin air, and igniting, exposing its purple blade. The Prince took a stance, and they began pairing against each other. The fight was long, and hard...

## Post 129 by Koru Tav on 2008-11-19T22:00:58Z



(edited)

Koru had bow armed as the prince worked on the lock. He peered into the dim light of the palace searching for a possible rear ambush as literally no enemy troops were around. He looked on the droid army and still barely could not get over how advanced they were. At a glance they seemed like the battle droids of old; however, if one stared for a second, one could find out that they were much different. The armor was reflecting the dim light. The armor looked like it could withstand a few rounds of blaster rifle fire before succumbing. He turned his head back to the prince and realized the easy time they had getting here: Alpha Point (Koru was sure) would be no cakewalk. Koru turned to face the doors as he heard the quiet machinery open.

As the door opened he could hear the sound of combat boots on the floor as well as the shouting of orders among other things. As the prince raised his hand ready to give the signal, he put his bow and arrow away and brought out his DL-44 and standard issue blaster pistol and aimed at an unfortunate soul who thought they were behind cover. Blaster fire poured into the princes ranks, the prince activated his lightsaber, and the princes troops counterattacked thus starting the battle for Alpha Point. That unfortunate soul was shot through the chest by one shot of Koru’s weapon. As Koru fired a few rounds per few seconds and was easily overshadowed by the sheer amount of laser blasts from the droid army.

After several minutes Stratus turned to Koru **,”Once Lord Kennedy is in sight, and you will know him instantly, stay out of the way, and get out of the immediate area and find other enemies to destroy, and guard the way from anyone else coming. Do NOT fire on Lord Kennedy, or you will die instantly. I must fight him alone."** Koru nodded and was about to resume firing when a dark presence filled the room and a man whom Koru assumed was Kennedy entered the area.

**“So that’s Kennedy…kind of old and ugly,”** Koru made his way to a close hallway, turned the corner, and turned to watch the ensuing fight. After a few words, tendril of electricity appeared out of nowhere and the battle between the two force users began. Koru watched with utmost fascination and respect as Stratus counterattacked. Koru blinked and squinted as he thought he something of a blue light eminate from the prince. Koru for some reason felt a reinforced sense of confidence as the two began their duel. Koru watched with amazement ,as he had never see force users in action, as the two duked it out. Flashes of purple and red illuminated the arena. The fight under different circumstances could have been considered a graceful dance of a warrior culture as the two attacked and counterattacked.

This was all in the first few seconds of the fight.

Out of habit, he pulled out his bow and an arrow knowing it. A shout brought him out of his stupor and he turned to the source just outside the boundaries of the arena. Six former royal guard stood there with their weapons. Koru brought his bow up, aimed, fired, and ran. After a few steps he took out a thermal detonator and dropped it on the floor to slow the enemy. Before the six could react, the arrow pierced one of the men and pinned him to the wall dead. The others began the chase and another was consumed by the explosion of a thermal detonator.

Koru ran through the hallways with enemy fire lapping on his heels. One grazed his side. Koru ignored the pain and kept running. A guard started to run out of one of the rooms. Without asking for his intentions, Koru curled right hand into a fist and struck the guard’s head running into the room where the now deceased guard was in. Koru took a split second to glance at the dead man. The helmet he was wearing was crushed where he struck the man. Seeing this Koru looked to his hand and noticed that the artificial flesh was gone revealing his robotic hand.  ***Must have hit him harder than I thought***. Koru made it to the back of the room, overturned a desk drew his bow, and waited. The room was similar to a library in the way that there was many bookcase type cases littering the room: in other words, lots of ambush areas. He also noted that there were several other dead bodies littered throughout the room.

He drew back the string as he heard footsteps and let it go as the first guard came around the corner. The arrow struck the man in the sternum forcing him into the rest of his comrades. Koru chose this time to seek new cover. Once situated, blaster fire poured through the table striking the wall. The blaster fire stopped and the sound of combat boots echoed softly throughout the room. Knowing that the bow would be useless in close quarters, Koru swapped it for his vibroknife. Several seconds passed until one of them came to where Koru was hiding. The guard looked down one of the aisles and then turned to Koru and seemed to jump in surprise as Koru knocked the enemy blaster rifle away from where it was pointing and kicked him in the kneecap. The man crumpled to one knee and was finished off with Koru’s knife. His mind screamed jump and Koru jumped into the next aisle as blaster bolts tore through where he was originally.

As Koru rose, looked at the wall and saw something similar to claw marks. They lead up to the ventilation shaft above him. ***How could anyone get up there? The only way someone could get up there is to vault up there*** He heard a noise in the shaft and looked up.

Blaster fire interrupted his thoughts as he was flung into the wall by the force of the blaster fire. Koru grunted as he hit the wall and came to a sitting position. The jolt flared the pain in his side. Koru didn’t any pain from the bolts and looked at his artificial right arm: It had several good size holes from impact and still had the smell of burnt circuitry. It was useless until he could get it repaired. He pulled out his DL-44 with his left hand and aimed it at the man that just came around the corner of the far end of the room.

**Koru**  The man said.

**Seth?!**  Koru asked with surprise. Seth was one of the few people he had considered to be friends with in the two weeks he had been there even though he hadn’t spent much time with him. **Why?**

**It’s none of your concern my friend** Seth emphasized the last word with sarcasm. **But since your gun hand is useless and because I am about to kill you…the Prince is a fraud. I’ve been in the Guard for a year and since he return…it’s been a downhill spiral. I was going to ask you to join our little coup but from the moment I saw you, I knew that you were a man of too much honor and duty. I decided to become your friend to follow an age old saying “keep your friends close and your enemies closer”. Through this I learned that you were a crackshot with that barbaric thing you call a weapon and that you were a great shot with your right hand…**

Koru looked at him ***The guy’s worse for words than I am.***

**…so now that you know, Good b-** Seth was cut off by Koru’s DL-44. Seth slumped to the ground.

**Pity you just watched instead of doing your research.** Koru picked himself up and made his way over Seth’s body to make sure he was dead. He quickly moved a few things and checked the vent and found it empty though the panel was loose. Fighting the effects of fatigue, he made his way back into the hallways, catiously searching for more enemies.

Less than ten minutes had passed since Koru fled into the hallways.

## Post 130 by James Stratus on 2008-11-20T06:18:04Z



The duel was getting more challenging. As Lord Kennedy somehow intensified the strength of his blade, the High Prince emulated his every move and did the same. He was a quick learner and was able to learn quickly the enemy's strategy and methods and duplicate them. As the Sith made a dark swing the High Prince's aura erupted in all directions, knocking Lord Kennedy and his swing out of the way, and the Prince himself, could not be seen. Lord Kennedy walked around a bit confused, lightsaber ready, searching out for the High Prince. His presence could no longer be detected through the Force.

Had the High Prince been defeated? Had that last swift stroke actually hit the High Prince and killed him, which resulted in the aftermath blast resulting from the very powerful Force-user's death? It had not seemed like it was an extreme enough aftermath to be the case, however, though anyone else would have assumed so...but the Dark Lord was highly intelligent.

**"Ah...had a run-in with the Fallanassi have we? Well well well....that is indeed impressive. It takes most adepts of their circle a much longer timeframe to master that technique, my old friend."**

When time which seemed like forever had passed, the High Prince's presence was re-established, except Kennedy now saw eight of versions of him, all which looked vaguely the same...almost.

**"You are correct in your presumption, Lord Kennedy."** The Sith Lord, unpreturbed by this, continued pacing and mocking the High Prince. One by one, the allusions slowly evaporated until only the true High Prince and the Sith Lord remained. The huge blue aura had returned surrounding the Prince, and the duel continued.

Next the typical Sith began using the Force to launch large and heavy objects in the direction of the Prince, all of which ended up being redirected at him, which required more concentration. **"You know, Sean....you are really beginning to make a big mess here. You've made quite a big mess already and I really don't want to have to clean up any more. It's starting to get on my** ***nerves!"*** he shouted, sending a huge invisible fist towards the Dark Lord. Lightsabers reactivated, and they continued paring and paring. The Sith Lord was able to extract the High Prince's lightsaber through a swift move, in which he became in possesion of the Prince's purple blade. Instantly a second lightsaber was in the Prince's hand, replacing the one lost, extracting a dark orange blade. He cringed at the sight of it. He really hated that color. The Prince made a mental note to construct a new backup weapon of a better color. This one had been made as an after thought, with the only gem that was available at the time. He was rather particular with his colors. They parried again and again, the one blade against the two in the hands of Lord Kennedy. Using a trick he had learned not only from Kennedy but combined with a skill taught to him by the Aing-Tii, the lightsaber in his hand retracted and was returned to its hiding place, while both lightsabers in Kennedy's hands, still ignited, appeared instantly in his hands, the red and purple blade crossing over each other. The Sith Lord reacted instantly hurling a huge wave of force at the Prince knocking him clear across the "arena", and proceded to send lightning in his direction. Using the lightsabers this time, the High Prince focused that energy into them deflecting the attack back in the direction of the Sith Lord.

## Post 132 by Revan Centurion on 2008-11-21T21:34:03Z



It would seem that the attention near the front side of the massive facility had distracted the vast majority of guards that Revan could run into. Yes, he was exercising discretion as much as possible, but he simply had the sneaking suspicion that the entire operation was just...going a bit too smoothly. Reaching yet another lengthy corridor, Revan crouched slightly as his danger senses prickled heavily. It happened almost too fast for him to even really be able to react. A brilliant flash at the end of the hall causes Revan to dodge low and to the right instinctively. The move, while extremely fast, just was not fast enough. Suddenly, he could feel his body gradually going limp. *This cannot be happening...* For what seemed like hours but was actually little more than a handful of brief moments, Revan struggled to regain control of his limbs through ordinary means.

As frustration began to course through his body, Revan opened himself to the full extent of the force. It was something he very rarely did - often giving him a force signature no greater than that of your typical trained Jedi Knight. Now, however, the Lord Protector of Naboo drew upon all of his training in both the light and dark side. There wasn't any real fantastic change in his appearance. Allowing the power of the force to surge through his body, Revan calmly and silently got to his feet. Cracking his neck from side to side, he pointed towards the ceiling with his right index finger. In an instant, the corridor was flooded with light from above. Finally able to see his foes in true form for the first time, Revan smiled. **"Ah...always best to put some light on the situation."**

The men standing between Revan and his objective did not laugh, smirk, or speak. Like the trained soldiers they were, they simply opened fire once more. Raising a hand, Revan created a large green bubble that absorbed the energy of the blaster fire, keeping it collected in front of him. When the soldiers realized what was happening, they stopped firing and looked on in confusion. Smirking, Revan released the full power of the ball of energy on to his stolid victims. In a mere moment, they were incapacitated. For now...they would remain alive. It was not presently Revan's job to pass judgment on their necessity for life. They presented no large detriment to the balance of the force at that moment. As such...he would permit them to search for their own manner of forgiveness.

When Revan did reach the door that he sensed to be the room containing Ashlee Stratus, Revan paused. Pulling the hood of his cloak back over the top of his head, he placed his left hand against the door. Willing the force to do his work, Revan sensed for presences within the room. There were six, one of them very, very distinct. Satisfied that Ashlee was not in the direct projectile path of the door, Revan let loose a powerful blast of the force from his left hand. The sheer velocity of the door flying inward instantly killed one of the five guards. Following no more than half a second behind the door was Revan himself.

As he jumped high into the air of the falling, limp body of the impacted guard, Revan's silver blade came to life, silently. Gripping the weapon tightly with his right hand, Revan used his left hand to raise one of the remaining four guards high into the air. As the body of the guard levitated some fifty meters ahead of him, he flung his lightsaber directly into the man's heart. The moment his feet touched the ground, he called the lightsaber back to his right hand and spun to strike down yet another guard. His swift, deliberate actions continued until all of the five guards were dispatched. To Ashlee, it would look like little more than a dark, cloaked figure surrounded by a blur of silver. The entire process took no more than twenty seconds. Efficiency was all that mattered to Revan. Unfortunately, he could not spare the lives of these guards. He knew all too well that attempting to incapacitate all of them would have likely only caused Ashlee to be executed.

Silently, Revan approached the young Princess with his lightsaber still activated and pointing downwards to the right. Stopping mere inches in front of her body, Revan deactivated the weapon before speaking in a low rumble of a voice. **"We need to go..."** Revan waved his left hand briefly, unfastening her restraints. **"They made the mistake of thinking I could be stopped by a small number of men, or they simply used them to draw me into a false sense of security. Either way...more men will be coming. Come with me, Princess."** Revan said nothing as he turned from Ashlee, making his way back to the door-less entrance. His mission was to get her out safely, and he would not fail. There would be time to answer questions about who he was later.

**Lord Revan Centurion**

*Praetorian*, Imperial Republic

*Lord Steward of Naboo*

*Shadow Jedi Master*

## Post 133 by Malia Jae'Nor Hollis on 2008-11-21T21:44:06Z



Mali crouched behind some rubble that had once been part of the wall. Maybe the door, even. In the end, the scortch marks from the initial blast and the dueling had made its permanent mark, and most of the entrance was now unrecognizable as the Coruscanti Palace. Her hand tremmered slightly, and she lowered it, favoring her right hand out of her own natural dominance, and the acceptance that she was not completely healed with the other. The day had been long already, and she had not had much sleep. But neither had most of the soldiers with her. Just returning to Coruscant after battle and a few days of leave, their schedule had been demanding and gruesome. And she was feeling it now.

Her left hand rested on the wall, still holding the now warm blaster, as she steadied herself and took aim again, hitting another trooper. He fell to the ground with a simple *thud*.

But now there was a new danger. The walkers. They could not enter the building, and thank the stars for that. But they were doing a fantastic job of holding her and the Soldiers inside, preventing them from even seeing what else was beyond the current danger. Mali wished she had brought her entire set of Mandalorian Armor. It would come in very handy about now. Just the jacket could only do so much.

She checked her stash of arsenal in her bag, and came up with six thermal detonators, three flash detonators, a Charric, Four grappling hook blasters, and four sonic detonators. Not much, but enough to come up with a plan. She called out for those nearest her to come closer, and she handed out her arsenal of explosives with very clear instruction.

The first unlucky set of soldiers were tasked with using the flash detonators to distract the remaining troopers and the operators of the gigantic walkers. As soon as the three detonators go off (in succession to give more time, not all at once), a third soldier would run out with their dark visor down and prepare to use the Charric to neutralize the armor of the troopers, so they may be taken out easier. While this was happening, Mali and three others would run out and use the grappling hook blasters, two per walker, and attach themselves to the bottom of the walkers, setting the thermal detonators to go off in 3o seconds, then get the hell away from it all as fast as they could. Not completely foolproof, but enough to make Malik think it could work. She took a deep breath, then gave the orders to continue ...

## Post 135 by Dene Vye Cognatus on 2008-11-22T07:29:11Z



As Dene decided on his own that he was fit for battle, he went back in the front lines and began firing with his own hands at the enemy soldiers approaching. There weren't many left of them. It was the walkers that were left that were hitting the front of the Palace that were really starting to get on his nerves. **"Colonel Haymon, get rid of those walkers! Target them now with everything you've got and fire! I want them destroyed,** ***now*****!."**

"**Yes General! Immediately sir!"**

He watched as the heavy cannons that had finally joined his reinforcements opened fire on the enemy vehicles, in addition to Colonel Haymon's own assault vehicles, taking them out one-by-one until they were almost all gone. He launched a few detonators at the one nearest him and watched as it took a big hit and fell to the ground. He quickly ran to it and began cutting through it's armor with his lightsaber while no one could see, and then finished off the two soldiers inside, then returned to the battle, his jedi weapon once again hidden. He did not want it known to the public that he was a Jedi, only to the trusted members of the High Council who knew of that fact did he allow to know. It was more for the protection of the public that they did not know. The last thing they needed was to think that a bunch of Jedi ran the government. In part it was true, though there were many non-Jedi running all aspects of the government as well. The public did not know that, however. There were perhaps rumours of the High Prince's force abilities, perhaps it was publicly known, he wasn't sure really what the public did know, but he did know that they *didn't* know about him and most of the others.

Thankfully only those two transports from the enemy had gotten through before the shield was activated. Otherwise he would have probably 4 times the problem to deal with, as there were about 4 more transports that were launched but did not make it to the surface. The defending fleet eliminated them, and not a moment too soon. Locke sure was doing a good job up there. He hoped that he made it through this battle. It was taking a heavy toll he knew on the fleets in orbit. They had lost a lot of good men and women over the last 24 hours.

He watched as the last of the enemy vehicles and soldiers were dispatched by the heavy cannons and assault vehicles. What a huge mess they had made of all the buildings nearby. He sighed. War is hell.

He made way back to the command post and began to look over the final reports. Lieutenant Hollis was still caught up in a bit of a trap, but the war was almost over. And for that he was greatful. He got on his comlink. **"Colonel Haymon, send a few reinforcements to report to Lieutenant Hollis. She is still engaged in conflict. They will report to her and eliminate the remainder of the enemy asap. Also send some reinforcements to the other shield generator and control locations right away."**

He signed off as the Colonel acknowledged and began viewing more data on the battle as it slowly came in, logistics and other officers finally now able to start filing reports and gathering data. It was a very important job indeed.

## Post 136 by Kaidlen Shan on 2008-11-22T15:29:51Z



(edited)

As red and ultraviolet blades met in frantic combat, Kaidlen could tell that the creature before her felt that he could take her. However, she had yet to open up on him fully with all of her extensive training. She felt that there was no need to do so at this time. Anyways, she would need her strength later on as well, in order to see that the objective was accomplished.

Unexpectantly a presence washed over her, strong in it's feeling. The Colonel knew that presence, had known it for most of her entire life. The earliest memories that she could recall were strong with that presence. Without warning, it ended. As it vanished, grief slammed down on Kaidlen. An inhuman cry of agony burned in her throat, and tried to escape past her lips. Instead, a somewhat gagging sound issued forth, as a trickle of dark red blood trickled down from the corner of her mouth. When the Sith in front of her began to speak, Kaidlen narrowed her eyes in irritation.

**"I am Shadow. I will never join the likes of you and your Master."** Kaidlen snarled. **"I'll die first!"** Her anger and her pain flowed through her, but she shunted them aside. Shadow tried to keep the Balance. She had been raised that way and would not dishonor the man who was her father in all but blood in that way.

The bright flash began and they all ran out to complete the task. One soldier, holding the Charric, targeted the Troopers. Mali thought it would be effective if the armor was ceramic based, but to her great relief, she heard screams of pain. Apparently, the metal armor on the Troopers had the shock effect rumored, but never thuroughly tested by the government. Until now. She made a mental note to include that in her report.

Mali and the three with the grappling blasters divided themselves to their assigned walkers, and just as the second flash detonators were going off, they blasted up to the joints between the legs and hit the retractor buttons, raising them up to the bellies of the machines. She heard fire blasts below, and knew without having to watch that the last of the target Troopers were being permanently delt with.

"Save one. I want one alive." She called down.

They set the detonator's timers and attached them. "T-minus thirty seconds, on Gamma!" She called, warning the others to be ready. She held her hand down, hand in a tight fist. The signal that she was ready. When all fists were down in a fist, she called the warning, and at once, they all pressed the triggor buttons. In lieu of taking too much time, they decended with the grappling hooks about half way down before jumping and running. They ran away from where they had come, in order to avoid being caught in the same situation, in favor of containmnet of the enemy. The other soldiers had set up a line of defense in safe proximity from the area, and they all just made it behind the line in time for the explosions.

The walkers exploded in an orange glow, they came crashing down, the large bulk crashing into the building. Mali gave the orders to fire on sight, but nothing was moving. The size of the walkers being reduced to the size of a medium oil drum gave her little cause to believe that anything would be walking away any time soon.

As she was reaching for her comm device, some Army transports containing more troops arrived. Mali moved to greet them.

"Lieutenant Hollis, I was sent by Colonel Haymon for assistance, but it looks like this is under control."

"Yes, it is. But I would feel more comfortable if we get more protection on the inside."

"Much agreed. Do you have the two exact locations?"

"I do."

She divided up the new troops into three groups. The smallest would stay outside and secure the entrance, and stick around just in case the enemy was not completely eliminated. The other two groups were to go in and secure the control areas, and await further instruction.

![malia-sig.jpg](https://images.eotir.com/sigs/malia-sig.jpg)

**The willing, Destiny guides them; the unwilling, Destiny drags them** (Seneca: Roman philosopher, mid-1st century AD).

SIDIOUS:I have waited a long time for this moment my little green friend.Huch huch hu at last the jedi are no more.

YODA:Not if anything to say about it i have!

## Post 139 by Koru Tav on 2008-11-23T19:23:34Z



Lt. Tav rubbed his jaw as he picked himself off the floor. He shook his head from the shock of being delivered a sudden kick in the head and headed toward the exit and chase down whoever had kicked him. As he shot a surprised enemy that came around the corner he quickly thought on how he came into this interesting situation and he sprinted after the figure who had turned into the adjacent hallway.

\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_

He had left Seth’s dead body and roamed the hallways doing cleanup. Along the way he had been picking any arrows he had fired. He had found most of them. It was while he was doing this that he heard a noise in the room next to him. He saw several dead enemies in the room: one seemed to have been pierced by something, one seemed to have been shot, one seemed to have had his neck broken by being clubbed too hard, and the other seem to have died from deep scratches on the throat.  *Interesting and weird*. He heard somewhat of a heavy panting and mumbling coming from the back corner of the room. He had carefully crept to where the noise was coming from and found someone holding his head. Though the figure looked human there was something different…

**“Stay away…stay away…stay out…”**

At first impression he thought the person was speaking to him but quickly deduced that this was not the case. **You ok?**  Koru asked as he looked to the door for a quick second.

**Get out…get out…get out!!!** The last phrase was shouted and the person’s eyes opened.

**Wha-**Koru was cut off from a sudden kick to the head. He hit the ground and faintly heard his attacker pick something off the floor and run for the hallway.

\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_

He chased the person through Alpha Point and he noted that he did not come into contact with any enemy soldiers. Though the Prince’s counterattack was pushing back the enemy, there still were enemy personnel throughout Alpha Point.

Koru’s chase led him to another room which ironically was the same room where he had his confrontation with Seth. He heard the sound of metal with ferracrete and suddenly everything clicked: the claw mark the loose vent. Maybe this person wasn’t part of the enemy force. As he entered the room he saw the vent open and saw the same figure gracefully land on his feet on the floor.

**I apologize for kicking you…I didn’t mean to do it…you just frightened me…this place just freaks me out…it’s filled with evil…don’t let him control me again…**

Koru’s eye raised at this person’s quick babbling. Either this guy was crazy enough to act like this to lie through his teeth or this was the real thing.

![korutav-sig.jpg](http://images.imperialrepublic.org/sigs/korutav-sig.jpg)

## Post 140 by Jaylen Myst on 2008-11-24T02:05:14Z



(edited)

OOC: sorry for the long post

\*\*\*Somewhere in Alpha Point at the time right before the fight between Stratus and Kennedy begins\*\*\*

**What were the orders again, sir?**

**I thought I made myself quite clear**. The person said with an annoyed sigh as he fingered the staff on his back Find anything of value. **Now hurry and carry them out and don’t have me repeat myself.**

**Yes, sir. Sorry sir.** The soldier took a quick second to glance at the “commander” and then went back to carrying our his orders. The “commander” seemed to disappear in dim light because of his skin color and what he was wearing; yet one could catch the glint of yellow from his eyes. However in full light, one would notice a human form and black clothing. The soldier hated him because he wasn’t fully human. “Commander” Jaylen Myst had cat traits as well as human. His skin color was gray with some black stripes. The ears where somewhat pointed with back at the tips. The eyes were what creeped him out the most: they were yellow feline type eyes. The soldier shuddered as he worked.

Jaylen stared at the door as if in a trance. Because of the gift of telepathy, he could feel the emotions of the men that were working in the room. He expanded his awareness and could tell where everyone else was in Alpha Point. He suddenly detected several more minds at the entrance of the base, one of which was very strong. He sent a mental message to Kennedy, **“ Lord Kennedy, we have guests…I believe one of them is the Prince.”**

**I know this…do not worry, I will dispose of him and his band…continue your previous orders.** And that is what Jaylen did. He really did not have a choice in the matter: he was a under a strong force control which was place on him a few years before against his will.

He felt the enemy move in and the sudden disappearances of consciousnesses of now dead soldiers. It didn’t affect Jaylen in the least; he was under his master’s control and his master didn’t care for a few deaths. A few seconds passed before a sudden burst of power in the force rushed through the room. Jaylen staggered and felt in his mind the pain of everything rushing through is mind as a veil-like presence lifted from his mind. Jaylen knew his mind was free.

**I…I’m…I’m free.** Jaylen said aloud as he almost broke down as he felt the evil presence of his former master in the base. Immediately feeling much fear, he felt the few men in the room turn toward him. He felt their threatening emotions. Without hesitating or looking where he fired, he immediately unholstered his pistol and shot the four men with four blasts in a little more than a second. Feeling even more fear from the vanishing of their presence combined with the with the fast approaching presence of more men he opened a vent and vaulted into it using his staff.

Over the course of the next few minutes, he witnessed one of the royal guard member bring down several other royal guardsmen with a knife and bow. He begin to fell more fear as the man checked the wall then look up to the vent. Jaylen closed his eyes as to prevent the man from seeing his eyes. He then felt the man’s surprise as he was shot several times in the right arm though was surprised when he did not feel the emotion of pain. He saw the shooter, at the other end of the room through the vent. He didn’t want the injured man to be shot and wanted to help him, but the fear of Kennedy’s presence pushed him farther into the shaft and out into another room. Shoving off any emotion coming from the ensuing killing he ran into the next room only to be fired upon. Again, thanks to his telepathy, he felt their intents and avoided the blaster fire.

Years of training and experience took over as he quickly brought out his staff and struck the soldier closest to him. Immediately, he flipped to avoid more blasterfire running the second guy through the the butt of his staff. The last radiated surprise and shock to the point of not moving. Jaylen pressed a button on his staff. The front open slid open to reveal a blaster barrel that was used to finish of the last man. Once the blaster barrel was hidden he hid in the far corner of the room and hid himself. Suddenly the large positive presence diminished into nothing but a echoing murmer (this is when Stratus pretends he’s dead and makes multiple images). Another burst of emotions of controlled anger and grief was detected from far away (this would be Kaidlen Shan emotional outburst). He quickly guessed the reason behind the outburst and tried to send a mental message of hope and encouragement. He doubted it successful because of the distance but tried anyway because of her openess in her anger. He stopped while he was sending the message as he could feel the tentacles of evil start to try to gain control again and bring the veil over his mind once again. He dropped his staff, fell to his knees, and clutched his head.

**No…you will not take control again…never…get out of my head…get out of my mind.** The tentacle tried to squeeze tighter.

**“Stay away…stay away…stay out…”** Jaylen faintly registered another voice. **“You ok?”**

**Get out…get out…get out!!!** As he shouted the last words the tentacles broke and withdrew. He also noted the positive presence had return. He opened his eyes and stared at the royal guardsman who he thought had been killed by the one named Seth. In sudden desperation, surprise, and fear he kicked the man in the chin, grabbed his staff, and fled the room. It wasn’t hard to avoid Kennedy’s troop because of telepathy. His fleeing brought him back to the room with the vent. He bolted back to the vent and hid. He saw the man stop in the entrance of the room and thought he registered the emotion of comprehension. He also did not feel any threating emotions.

Finally mustering up enough willpower Jaylen decided to face the man. T*his is stupid. Why am I scared of one man: because of my former master.* He thought those word with fear and a twinge of bitterness. *I can kill him if he decides that he wants to kill me.* With those final thoughts, he jumped from the vent onto the floor and began speaking, “**I apologize for kicking you…I didn’t mean to do it…you just frightened me…this place just freaks me out…it’s filled with evil…don’t let him control me again…**

He stopped in mid sentence feeling mistrust and suspicion. **I know that you’re thinking that I’m crazy and that I’m not telling the truth…I guess I am crazy in a way…but I’m telling the truth…**he stopped again midsentence feeling the pressure of confusion and fear**…I know because I have telepathy…just please don’t let him control me anymore…**He stopped as he felt a hand touch his shoulder. He looked up into the man purple eyes and saw a small smile.

**Come on kid, Let’s get outta here.** The man turned to the door and then realized why the man had not felt any pain even with three holes in his arm: his right arm was robotic. Walking quickly to catch up he spoke again for the reason that he could speak with a free mind **My name is Nox…no…that’s a nickname…my name is Jaylen Myst.**

The man stopped and turned his head to face Jaylen **Koru. Koru Tav. By the way, that was a nice kick that you pulled back there. Thought you broke my jaw back there.**

A compliment?! He couldn’t remember the last time he had been given a good compliment. As they reached the door to the hallway, the man he now knew as Koru switched gears into professionalism **Stick close kid. This ain’t gonna be a cakewalk.** Koru paused **Perhap, if you want, you could use that telepathy help us steer clear of an real danger.**

Jaylen nodded his head with uncertainty as the duo plunged into the hallway for a second time.

**Edited November 24, 2008 by Jaylen Myst**

## Post 141 by Marc Stratus on 2008-11-24T05:07:23Z



Aboard the Imperial Republic Omega-*class* Cruiser *Vanguard*, Prince Marc Stratus stood on the command deck with Captain Travis Gourdine, his brother-in-law. Marc had managed to escape the seige of Coruscant with his sister Nicole, and the two split as soon as they cleared the system, Stratus Security Agency having provided emergency transportation to them both, and one of them heading for Corellia, the other meeting up half-way with an outer-rim perimeter fleet. A lot of normal....procedures or laws were being broken. Captain Gourdine, no doubt very concerned about his wife, the Princess Ashlee, had remained at his post and knew his duty, though was ordered to the borders of the Hapes Cluster. The rest of his unit had remained behind, all but his ship. While the Hapans would not be thrilled, it was clear they were not attempting any kind of attack with a single Omega Cruiser, and it was also clear that it had become a diplomatic escort vessel, commandeered by the Royal Family. Marc had been a member of the Hapan Royal Family and still had some minor influence on Hapes, though with the new Queen Mother's take over, there was no telling. They had heard reports that the Executor had been taken hostage, and he would not allow more victories on the enemy's part. He had the full authority of the Royal Family backing his actions and was taking no actions against Hapes, only against those who held her hostage. Captain Travis Gourdine, though very, very, reluctantly, followed the direct orders given to him by the Prince. The Prince had only intefered with military affairs on Kuat, and very little anywhere else, though he did have a lot to do with naval matters all around, being in charge of ship production. He was, perhaps, the only one who had authority to do what they were now doing, in both governments. He would not interfere at all in any way with the government on Hapes, and he seriously doubted it was the Hapan government holding her hostage. In fact, he was sure they would probably be attempting to help her. Captain Gourdine stood there, shaking his head, as the Hapan fleet opened a channel.

**"Captain, to avoid any conflict with the Hapes Consortium, your ship will remain here. I will go in a diplomatic shuttle with no escort to avoid military conflict. My brother got her into this mess and I am going to get her out of it."**

"**With all due respect, Your Highness, you are not going there alone. I am glad that you finally decided not to invade their territory unannounced and unarranged. But I will be going with you in that shuttle, with a small squad of my finest. Their the ones who go to all the ships we have to board."**

The Prince looked as if to refuse, but decided not to offend his brother in law. **"Just be careful, Travis. Ashlee will kill me if anything happens to you."**

The Captain laughed. **"What right does** ***she*** **have to get mad at** ***either of us*** **for going off into dangerous situations, hm?**

As the shuttle arrived at Hapes, they immediately offered identification. **"Hapan Fleet, this is the Imperial Republic Diplomatic Shuttle** ***Peace Maker*****, bearing a member of the Royal Family of the House Stratus, His Royal Highness, the Prince Marc. We request a military escort and permission to land at the Royal Palace of Her Esteemed Majesty the Queen Mother of Hapes. We respectfully await your response."**

There was a long pause, and finally their Hapan friends responded. **"This is an unexpected visit. We will however accomodate His Highness for a short stay only. Permission is granted. Proceed on your present course. A military escort is on its way. I trust I don't have to tell you to follow it. We will be ensuring you are who you say you are prior to allowing you to enter the Palace, for Her Majesty's safety and security."**

**"Acnkowledged. We will submit to any security screening you require and will fully cooperate in the interest of Her Majesty's protection."**

As they landed, a large and heavily armed Hapan security team awaited them, partly an honor guard, and part to make sure they didn't cause trouble. They allowed the armed men of the Prince's team to accompany him "as his guards" but after closely examining their weapons and confirming that he was indeed who he claimed to be. Prince Stratus reached out through the *Force.* Though Tavria was not *Force*-sensitive, she had a very distinctive and unique presence in the *Force*, as did all individuals, though it was easier to identify people they knew and had associated with a lot better. He could sense her, and she was a bit alarmed. That was not good. He could not detect Layha anywhere, though he was not surprised. Although Marc himself was a powerful Jedi Master, Layha was even more powerful than he was, and he knew she often hid her presence from detection. She could be anywhere. He didn't know what to think, but all he knew was he was going to get the Duchess out of here and leave everyone esle alone. He did not want to cause any tensions or problems with Hapes and the Imperial Republic. He actually had a lot of love and respect for Hapes, having spent most of his life here growing up in the Royal Family of Hapes at the time, having been adopted by the then Queen Mother when his own mother, who was best friends and a relative of the Queen, passed away. They had fled to Hapes for refuge from the uprising and insurgents on Kuat who had killed his father and were after them.

He brushed that all aside, and then he explained briefly and extensively to the Hapan military leader who had come to greet him personally, all that had taken place on Coruscant with the guards, with the Sith, and what he had felt with the Executor, and explained his real purpose here. The general acknowledged that he knew something was up, even on Hapes, that had him concerned, but he had not official word except not to interefere with the diplomatic delegations in any way. **"Her Majesty left me strict instructions. I'm sorry, Your Highness. I cannot interfere, otherwise I would. I won't, however, stop you from interfering. Her Majesty said nothing about that,"** he said with a smile. He then gave some orders and all of the Hapan troops dispersed. **"I will leave a small team of my trusted men behind to guard your ship. I give you my word nothing will happen to it.**"

The Prince exchanged salutes with the General. They had been friends, a long time ago. Now they were distant, though not enemies in any way. The general was a noble leader, though he followed orders of every Queen Mother with exactness, no matter what they might be, and at one point those actions had been against Marc, from a corrupt Queen. The general regretted it but he did what he was told, but now they were still on equal grounds. Marc turned to his men and gave the signal. He had a feeling help from another source was on its way. An unusual source. Intelligence informed him another had been assigned to the case, but had so far failed to arrive safely in the system, but was still on its way. He only hoped they remained anonymous and did not give themselves away.

**Prince Marc Stratus**

*Duke of Kuat*

House Stratus

## Post 144 by Marc Stratus on 2008-11-24T19:42:02Z



Just before they had taken off, the general had tossed a small datacard to the Prince and winked at him. Marc pulled out his datapad as he always had it on hand and slid in the card. It was encrypted, and Marc thought of a code that the general and himself had used when they were kids. He punched it in and sure enough, it unlocked the card. The card had the location where the general believed the Executor was last known, and outlines of the area that could lead them to where she was being held. Marc could feel her presence and as he was guided he was able to isolate it to a smaller grid to a general area where she was most likely to be. **"Captain, I believe she is being held prisoner here."** He pointed to the spot on the datacard. **"This could get tricky, stay alert."** He lead the way. He knew the Hapan Palace well, as he had spent a good portion of his life in it. He was able to lead them to the area where the diplomatic discussions had taken place. The all krept along the sides of the wall as they peaked through a passage way. There were armed guards. Marc could distinctly make out the difference between the Hapan guards and the traitorous Imperial guards they had encountered on Coruscant. He knew a Hapan when he saw one. The trouble was, how would the Hapan guards react? They would probably retaliate thinking they were under attack. **"Set your weapons for stun, all of you. I don't want a single Hapan to die today. Kill the Imperial royal guards we encountered on Coruscant if necessary. Do not let the Hapans come to any major harm. It makes things a whole lot more complicated. Your focus will be on stunning the Hapans. I will take care of the enemy guards myself. Now quick, jam their communications. The time to act is now."**

The Prince nodded, giving the awaited signal, and the elite space troops opened fire on the Hapan guards, stunning them, while Marc, lightsaber ignited, deflected the incoming bolts and sending them towards the enemy troops, not the Hapans. The hapans were a bit confused as they saw the Prince who they recognized cut down the "IR royal guards" and being very careful not to allow their own to become harmed in any way other than being stunned. One of them even stopped firing and held up his weapon, and the Prince seized it and began firing at the enemy guards. He left the Hapan to stand their confused. Finally the Hapan shouted. **"What the blazes is going on, Your Highness? Why are you attacking your own and making sure we are not harmed?"**

Another had stopped firing and the one who had spoken to Marc slumped to the ground, having been hit by a laser bolt from the imperial guards, who had decided to start attacking the hapans as well. This served Marc well, as they began retaliating against the Imperial guards and Marc and his troops began focusing on the Imperials rather than the Hapans as the hapans gave aid to the Royals. When all the enemy guards had fallen, the Prince in a very commanding tone demanded to know where the Executor was. "**Where is she?! Tell me now!"** He commanded.

**"She...is being held by your own guards in that room."** One of the officers pointed to a blast door that was heavily shielded. Without saying a word the Prince rammed his lightsaber into the blast door, holding it there strong as the area his saber was began to cut through and melt the strong armor. It was not easy and took a long time to cut through, but it did work. No doubt those inside, including Tavria, would see him cutting through.

**Prince Marc Stratus**

*Duke of Kuat*

House Stratus

## Post 146 by Koru Tav on 2008-11-24T23:41:53Z



Koru and his companion made their way through the hallways with relatively little resistance. They eventually few seconds of firefight with at least five former royal guards that could not be avoided. Thankfully he was warned in advance by Jaylen. The battle was harder with Koru's right arm hanging limp at his side. Jaylen was a totally different story. When the firefight had begun, he cowered in the corner. The kid didn't know when to make up his mind: be scared of fight. He wasn't angry with him. In the heat of battle it always helped when your allies helped. Koru stuck his head out and finished off the few shots left in his DL-44. He heard a grunt as he took cover once again, his right hand hitting the was with a metallic klink. He quickly realized that was the last of his DL-44 ammunition. He dropped the heavy blaster pistol and managed to unholster his regular issue blaster pistol. He fired off three shots before ducking back behind cover. As the enemy blaster fire passed over head, he remembered his backpack. He quickly took out a thermal detonator, armed it, waited a second and a half, and threw it toward the enemy's position.

**Take Cover!!!**  The detonator exploded filling the hallway with screams and smoke. How effective it actually was he didn't know.

**Jaylen! Move it.** Koru yelled as he sprinted down the hallway a few yards into a room. He quickly surveyed the room: a few heavy desks, some cabinets, and a door at the other side of the room. The desk would last for awhile. The cabinets were garbage. The door presented a problem. He quickly ran to the door, locked it and proceeded to turn one of the desks on its side. Jaylen came next to him and helped him with his task, the traitor started firing into the room. They both immediately took cover. Unfortunatly, felt a wave of pain from his side as he finished taking cover. He gritted his teeth as he threw his final thermal detonator. One of the blaster shots had grazed his side. He couldn't deaden the pain at all or hold his side as only one of his arms worked. The smell of burnt flesh was actually a minute blessing as it made him realized that the wound was cauterized. If it was a projectile...he'd be in trouble. He fired off a few more shots actually hitting one in the head. As he fumbled to reload his pistol with one hand he looked back over to the cowering form of Jaylen. *Whatever happened to him must of been serious.*  He quickly popped up and shot one of the guards that tried to make his way into the room then took cover once again.

**Jaylen, you need to snap out of it!!!** Koru yelled as he fired off several more rounds that killed another one before taking another hit in the right arm. He suddenly felt off balance as he ducked back into cover. He looked up to see the Jaylen staring at the floor: there was his metal arm. Koru rolled his eyes and fired off a few more round before reloading again. **At least it won't get in the way** he mumbled sarcastically. He realized that he was on his last cartridge. He then realized that if Jaylen didn't snap out of it or if they didn't get help soon. It was not going to be pretty. His mind suddenly went to the det packs in his pack. It was an insane idea. They were too close; even with the table in the way, Koru wasn't sure that they would come out unscathed. As said before, the idea was insane...but Koru hadn't lived this long with following insane plans. He took out the det pack, set it for 5 seconds, activated it then threw it with all his might through the hallway to where the enemy was taking cover. As he threw it he ignore the pain in his side. Without thinking he threw himself on Jaylen.

He faintly registered a shocking scream come out from the hallway as the det pack exploded.

## Post 153 by James Stratus on 2008-11-27T04:18:23Z



Aboard the *Avenger* Vice Admiral Tinkel watched the tactical displays when he had a sick feeling in his stomach. It was the same feeling he had before he had ordered the evacuation of the *Prince's Pride.* He had little authority over this vessel. He did not have the Force or any inklings of it, though he did have gut intuition which had helped him in some battles. If only it had been there for all of them. He turned quickly to the admiral commanding *this fleet*. **"Admiral Sarriak I advise you to abandon ship immediately. This ship is about to be destroyed. I know the capabilities of that Super. I recommend you put it on a collision course and evacuate the ship. My shuttle is standing by, I would like you to come with me. Send the troop transports down to the planet after you load them to their maximum capacity - they will be safer there and able to reinforce the ground troops. I have taken the liberty of ordering additional transports and shuttles from my fleet to come empty-handed to fill with as many of your men as possible."**

Rear Admiral Sarriak stood silent for a moment. He looked as if to utterly refuse, but then he saw the tactical display and began to have a similar feeling. **"I am afraid you are right."** he said very softly. **"Captain, hardwire a collision course to do the most damage on that Super, and order the evacuation of this vessel. All hands, Abandon Ship!"** The captain could not believe what he was hearing. He, like the commander of the Prince's Pride, wanted to go with the ship, however the Admiral refused. **"You will come with myself and Admiral Tinkel, that is a direct order."**

The evacuation was quick and smooth, thanks to Admiral Tinkel's advance planning. He opened a channel to Admiral Firecam's flagship. **"Admiral, we are preparing to abandon the** ***Avenger*****. I'm afraid its a loss, Sir. I am taking the officers in my shuttle. What are your orders concerning where we should go?"**

They launched away as the *Avenger* began on a collision course at ramming speed, and was completely destroyed as it struck the Super Star Destroyer.... It was not a total loss, however, as it caused enough damage to cause the Super Star Destroyer to self-implode.

## Post 155 by Revan Centurion on 2008-11-27T04:39:12Z



Revan did little more than nod his head simply to the young Princess. As they entered the corridor, Revan motioned for her to follow quickly and silently as they slinked down the long hallway. It wouldn't do to attempt taking her back out the way he had come in. By now, Revan felt as if an overbearing presence had been lifted off his shoulders. It was as if darkness in the air had dissipated suddenly and quickly. Naturally, Revan could assume that meant Kennedy was either dead or otherwise incapacitated, but he was not so quick as to make that assumption. Besides - even in death, Kennedy could become more powerful than ever. Revan had experienced it first hand with his own relative...

After little more than three minutes of calculated, deliberate movement, Revan and Ashlee had arrived just outside the entrance to the small hangar which housed vessels designed for emergency escape. Since Revan had initiated a lockdown on that area of the facility and overloaded every security terminal in the building, he would be forced to do things the old fashioned way. Allowing the power of the force to wash over him, Revan focused on the heavy door before him. Several severely strong pushes of the force and the door had been bent inward enough to allow both individuals passage to the other side.

Naturally, Revan went through first. Much to his relief, he only found three guards waiting there. It was a quick matter of attacking each in a mind-blurring flurry of movement with his lightsaber. Wasting little time, Revan still had yet to say anything to Ashlee. Boarding one of the waiting shuttles, Revan quickly took a seat behind the controls. What few weapons the vessel had simply would not be enough to blast their way out. However, the shuttle did have a remote connection to the compound's security system. At that moment, it was the only way for Revan to access any of the security protocols.

As Ashlee took a seat next to him, looking rather puzzled at the mysterious man that had come to her aid, Revan managed to slice into the network and disable the lockdown. With practiced experience, Revan activated the vessels repulsorlift engines. The shuttle did not need to clear the deck by any extraordinary degree. The moment the skids were clear, he engaged the sublight drive in full and retracted the landing gear. Rocketing through the escape tunnel before them, Revan pushed a button to activate the evasive radar display on the forward window. If nothing else, it would help to keep Revan alert of anything being fired at the vessel from a variety of distances.

*~James, I have Ashlee. We've left Alpha Point. I'm taking her out of the immediate engagement area. Advise when you've identified a location you'd like her extracted to.~*

Revan still said nothing to Ashlee.

**Lord Revan Centurion**

He had never used it in public, so nobody really knew what the color of the blade was, but his Noghri bodyguards were not surprised to see that the color was Navy blue.

He was not sure he recognized the man in front of him. He vaguely remember his face from an old classified report he had read several months ago, stating that he was dead and that former Executor Solo Katarn had killed him.
