The Admiral said that in a very neutral tone. He was not angry now, as he let all his emotions go. He knew that the Sith would try to use his emotions against him and he was prepared for that. Muurashkin launched a powerful blow from up to down, trying to cut him from head to toes, but the Admiral raised his lightsaber and parried the blow, releasing tons of sparks in the process. ## Post 160 by Marc Stratus on 2008-12-01T06:51:43Z



The Chiss were unusually friendly. Marc was a little nervous still. He sensed Tavria was too. He stayed close to Tavria. She had to be protected, and he was really the only one he trusted besides Travis that was there to do it. They soon arrived at the *Vanguard*.

**"Unidentified vessel, identify yourself."** the *Vanguard* was hailing them.

**"What an incredibly redundant statement."** One of the Chiss pilots commented. **"Captain Gourdine, you may want to contact your ship so it does not fire on us while you and the important guests are aboard."**

**"That is a good idea."** Captain Gourdine replied. He got on his comlink, since he was not allowed access to the cockpit. **"Vanguard, this is a uh...friendly vessel.**" he said very carefully, deciding that now was not the time to mention the word "Chiss", especially on an open channel. **"This is Captain Gourdine, and I am aboard the unidentified vessel. On my authority I do not require it to identify with you, due to the nature of the circumstances. The Executor and Prince Marc Stratus are aboard. Please allow us to dock immediately. Authorization Gourdine Beta Vector Seven."**

**"Unidentified vessel, you are now designated call name of Marine Two until the Executor is aboard the** ***Vanguard*****. Standby for military honor guard escort.**"

**"Acknowledged,** ***Vanguard*****."**

**"Captain, we have been monitoring your communications. I take it those fighters will mean us no harm? We must be promised a safe passage out of here after dropping you off."**

The captain eyed the executor. He did not really have any diplomatic authority, but as a Captain of an Imperial Republic warship he did have some in these reguards, though with her present that authority was yielded automatically. Out of respect he looked to her for a nod before he continued. She nodded at him, as did the Prince. **"Of course. We are forever greatful for your services provided, and hope to be able to perhaps open negotiations of some kind of peace with you at a later date."** He added, taking a little liberty, hoping the Chief Diplomat a few feet away from him agreed with that statement.

As the ship arrived in the docking bay of the *Vanguard*, a very large assembly of space and ground troops were all gathered at attention, and the first officer and armed stormtroopers approached the chiss vessel. They were quite the show. The large assembly would be helpful if the unknown people transporting the Executor's team decided to get in a fight, and if not, they would certainly be prepared to recieve such an honored guest. Never before had the *Vanguard* been visited by the Throne, though they had many visits from the captain's wife, Princess Ashlee Gourdine of the Royal Family. Captain Gourdine went first, followed by the the Executor, with the Prince Marc right behind with his hand on his lightsaber in case the Chiss or even traitors possibly aboard the *Vanguard* decided to strike. They were followed by the team of elite commandos that had accompanied them to Hapes from the *Vanguard*, as well as Commander Halcyon in the rear, who stayed in the Chiss vessel to thank them and spend a few moments with them before finally getting off onto the *Vanguard*.

The officers saluted their captain, and then offered salutes to the Executor and the Prince, offering a quick bow. **"Your Excellency, it is good to see you safe. We will make sure you are kept safe while you are aboard this vessel. We are at your service."** The officer eyed the captain, looking for some sort of sign as to who was now in command, if anyone beside himself.

Captain Gourdine caught on, and then looked at Prince Stratus and Executor Treyson. **"Your Highness, I know that you commandeered this vessel. Perhaps you can help me now as to who is in command, since the Executor is now aboard?"**

**"Captain, the Royal Family's authority is unique from the Throne, as the Throne's authority is unique from the Royal Family. The Royal Family's authority is superior only in event of a supporting vote of the other members. However, her authority is superior to my own. I yield command to the Executor. I do recommend, however,** ***Your Excellency*****"** purposely using formal titles and methods of address in the presence of others, especially the large gathering **"I officially recommend we head for Kuat, as it will most likely be safer while Coruscant is still under siege or in recovery. And after we arrive, I have a few more reccomendations for you,"** he added softly. **"What is your command?"**

**Prince Marc Stratus**

*Duke of Kuat*

House Stratus

## Post 161 by Dene Vye Cognatus on 2008-12-01T09:26:44Z



As the General got farther in his report, he saved it and returned to look at the tactical screens on the portable command center. Dene's thoughts were directed at the Palace. **"Colonel, rally up some troops together with demolitions and cutters. We're going to** ***storm the palace*****. Send me the stormtroopers."**

Colonel Haymon looked at the General with uncertainty, letting down a big *gulp*, swalling at nothing, and then nodded. He turned around and gave the order and the general was soon surrounded by an elite squad of stormtroopers. They were used in special scenarios and situations, as they were not as large in number as the Army itself, and they were indeed very well trained. They were perhaps the most advanced trained in combat equal to the training that the Royal Guard had to undertake. They were not expendable by any means. Only certain circumstances warranted their use, and Dene had been using them a lot during the course of this battle. **"You will stay here Colonel and take command of the ground forces outside the palace. I will lead the team in the Palace. You have command until you are able to get in contact with me. Hopefully we can get communications restored. Allright Stormtrooper Corps, follow me!"**

Dene didn't bother with the blast doors that were locked. Immediately he motioned for the cutters to come forward and he guided those using them with the *Force*, though they didn't know it, to cut stronger and deeper and precise, making the job a lot quicker and easier. As the cut was complete, they pushed their way through, and erupted in a fire-fight with the royal guards assigned to guard the blast doors. They were soon joined, however, by friendly cross-fire from the Palace Security teams taking position on the other side. They had been waiting for the Army to storm the palace, and in fact, had been counting on it. The conflict was quick, and Dene's army was joined by a good-sized team of heavily armed and fresh security officers, having been hiding and waiting. With just a few words to the General and a nod they fell into place immediately behind his stormtroopers, joining the fray, recognizing the General as their direct superior until the conflict was over and all settled. They believed him still loyal to the Throne, and were counting on that.

Dene was a tracker, that had been one of his skills he had picked up and specialized in during his own commando training. He carefully began to trace the movements of what appeared to have been the Palace Security and probably Security Bureau commando teams movements. It appeared they had gotten far. He sensed some dark presences very distant, and also a few faint traces of people he knew. Since his Force training, Dene had become an even better tracker. Combining the two skills together, Dene was able to get close to the Nexus Core in less than half the time it had taken OVERMIND's team, though in their defense they had no clue where they were going, and Dene's advantage was the nice trail they were leaving behind as they engaged in fights along the way, not to mention some blood. He knew he was getting closer. From a distance he could hear the shout of security teams and the battle going on not far ahead. It almost sounded like he could also hear lightsabers clashing. *That* couldn't be good at all. He picked up the speed, his troops not far behind him, and headed for wherever they were headed. He had never been in this part of the Palace before. In fact, he was pretty sure it was kept very secret, even from him, as he sensed it had a very important purpose, and now, here he was, with all these other people, battling it out, right in the center of it all. It was becoming very high tech as he came closer and closer. Something very important and critical was being run here. He had heard rumours of what people called the Core, the super-super computers that regulated much of the government's computer systems and Coruscant itself. Nothing like it that anyone knew of existed anywhere else, and most people believed it to be a myth, since the Imperial Republic's datacenters were spread all over Coruscant that as far as anyone knew, served that purpose. Perhaps this place was something more significant. He may still never really know, but he was about to get closer to whatever it was, and whatever was going on.

**President of the Senate**

**Supreme Chancellor of the Imperial Republic**

**Member of the Royal Imperial Throne**

**Jedi Master**

## Post 163 by Dene Vye Cognatus on 2008-12-01T18:09:49Z



Dene saw Kaidlen standing with her ligthsaber in action, though she was pretty much alone, only left with one of her Noghri guards. **"Quick, cover her!"** the general ordered. **"Colonel Shan! Can you give me an update?"** he asked. While she did not report to him, the chain of command was broken now and everyone was reporting to the most senior or highest ranking officer leading the troops, just as the PSA and IRSB had fallen in with the Grand Admiral.

*"General Cognatus, Admiral OVERMIND led a team of IRSB agents and PSA officers to the Nexus Core. It does exist, and it is vital...and it is controlling the jamming. He is on his way to take care of it, but he is encountering heavy resistance. I was with him but we were attacked by a Sith and I stayed behind to deal with him, but he...got away. I suspect he is dealing with uh...someone else now." she said, not wanting to reveal his force status. "I do believe Minister Benson is still being held hostage. Now that you are here to reinforce the Admiral, I can go and rescue Benson on the Ministry of Finance level."*

General Cognatus nodded and looked her over. **"You are injured, Colonel, you should get medical attention."** Colonel Shan raised an eyebrow as she looked at some serious battle wounds on the general. **"Uh..just some ...minor injuries, nothing serious..."** She tapped her foot and crossed her arms. The general conceited. **"Okay, so maybe this war has done a number on all of us. But we have to keep moving. You win. Proceed with your plan, however you will take a squad of mine with you."** He turned to one of his unit officers. **"Lieutenant Na'tor, you and your squad will accompany Colonel Shan. Until this war is over, you will report directly to her. Colonel Shan, I am placing this squad at your command for the duration of the war. Take care of them, now,"** he added. **"Alright, break off, time to get moving,"** he shouted to everyone. He saluted the Colonel. **"May the Force be with you, Colonel. And good luck."** With that he was off with his men behind him.

## Post 165 by Khyron Overmind on 2008-12-02T04:10:09Z



(OOC: I will have to make this short, since it is extending quite a lot)

As they were fighting, a large disturbance in the Force: a large presence was there and the next moment it was not. The Admiral tuned his senses to find out who had it been, as he knew the High Prince was near there. But he then felt the presence of the Prince and therefore, he realized that his enemy was the one that had just disappered.

Muurashkin felt it too and his face went blank for a moment. He had troubles trying to keep his composture, now that his master had apparently died. And the Admiral sensed it too, even though Muurashkin was still hitting hard with his lightsaber, and the Admiral was parrying and keeping him at bay. The moment of weakness passed quickly and then Muurashkin increased his attacks. Sparks and sparks were flying all over the place, as the two men kept fighting. Nobody would yield anything, but the fight was taking a toll on the Admiral: he was wounded and he realized that he would not be able to keep up with the fight for too long. Therefore, he needed a new strategy, and he needed it fast.

He focused on Muurashkin, trying to find a weakness in his mind, something that would allow him to find a way to defeat him. And then , he noticed it: he was a clon.

**"You are dead"** the Admiral said, between strikes. **"And now your master is also dead. You have no chance here and you will follow him soon."**

**"Brave words Admiral, brave words, considering that you are about to die. A while ago, you would have been a great addition to our group, since we could have used a man of your expertise. However, now my master has already killed your so precious Prince and we are ready to take over the government."**

And with that, he increased his attacks on the Admiral, who was finding it more and more difficult to defend himself. Until he finally realized what he had to do. He needed Muurashkin to get angrier than he was, so he became reckless and allow him a whole in his defense to kill him. And there was only one way to make him madder: he projected on Muurashkin mental images of his master being killed by the High Prince. He was no master at that, but being Murashkin angrier, he lowered his mental guard enough for the Admiral to take advantage of it. It worked.

As soon as Muurashkin increased his attacks, he became reckless and reckless, as the Admiral was showing signs of getting really tired, until he finally had an opening: he stroke hard and caught him on the middle of the stomach. The Admiral deactivated his lightsaber and watched as Muurashkin body fell on the floor, still keeping a face of surprise. He straightened his body and looked for the last time to his enemy. His energies seemed to come back at him: he had been overshowing his tiredness in an effort to lure Muurashkin into his trap.

The next part of his mission was relatively a piece of cake: he proceeded to the Nexus Core and restored communications.

![overmind-sig2.jpg](http://images.imperialrepublic.org/sigs/overmind-sig2.jpg)

## Post 166 by Locke Firecam on 2008-12-02T05:10:33Z



Looking out from the bridge of the *Highwind* the Admiral watched the battle near its conclusion as the final Super star destoryer burst into a million pieces. He shook his head, clearing his mind and returning to the task at hand. His captains were more then capable of finishing up the remainder of the battle, and he left it in their hands. Standing up from his seat, he moved over to the large tactical display nearby, pulling up the list of new arrivals. ## Post 167 by Dene Vye Cognatus on 2008-12-02T06:38:15Z



(edited)

Dene broke into a full run, and then arrived just in time to see the Grand Admiral defeat the Sith Lord. *It must have been some fight*, he thought, *for the admiral is all beat up and about to collapse.* He ran over to him and the admiral's lightsaber was hidden once again before Dene's stormtrooper regiment caught up with him. **"Admiral, you are hurt...you need..."** he cut himself off. Lets see, his own XO said that to him during the battle, he had said it to Colonel Shan who, without words said the same thing back at him, and now here he was saying it to his own CO. Typical. **"Well, what are your orders Admiral?"**

The admiral was tired and worn out. **"We must complete our mission,"** he said, with what breath he had left. He watched as the Admiral stood tall and strong, a new aura about him. He had grown in great strength in the *Force*, and if it werent for the Force right now he would have collapsed, and Dene probably a long time ago before that. He walked behind the Minister of Defense and watched in awe as the Grand Admiral swiftly cut off the remaining enemies, with General Bodost already in position, finishing off the last of the enemy guards there. *This general from the IRSB is quite impressive*, he thought. *Though this is probably giving him more combat experience for sure as it is us all.* He watched the Grand Admiral go to a special terminal, and, inserting his code cylinders, and with his voiceprint, watched as the communications were restored throughout the Royal Palace. Dene looked around. The place was a mess, but communications were back. He looked over at the Admiral and spoke in quiet respect. **"I never thought I'd see the day when a Grand Admiral of the Imperial Republic would lead a ground strike force and personally engage in personal combat against enemy troops."** He truly was in awe. He was quiet for a moment, as was the Admiral. They sat in silence as they took it all in. A lot of good men had died. They had been betrayed from deep within. It was a deep blow to them all. They had lost a lot of friends. The Admiral remained silent for a moment. Dene then added, with a slight grin, a little bit of his normal personality creeping in through the gloom. "**Aren't you getting a little old for this sort of operation, Admiral?"** he chided.

He opened a channel to Admiral Firecam, just as the announcement came through that the Admiral was stepping down as Regent, and that the Executor had returned to Imperial Republic space. This was good news...but where was the High Prince? He got on his comlink.

**"Admiral Firecam...this is General Cognatus. We have now cleared the ground of enemy forces, though there is much collataral damage. We are now inside the Palace and have met up with Admiral OVERMIND. The palace is...well....its not pretty...but communications have been restored, thanks to our Grand Admiral here. We will begin the search for the Supreme Ruler immediately...he uh...is still missing... and cleanup operations will begin shortly. How are things up in orbit?"**

## Post 168 by Kaidlen Shan on 2008-12-02T17:48:20Z



(ooc: Kaidlen doesn't have Nohgri bodyguards, she doesn't rate them. The Nohgri with her is a good friend.)

The Colonel nodded in agreement at the General's orders concerning the squad. She would need the troopers to rescue the Minister. Signalling to Neehmai, she looked over at the people just assigned to her.

**"Who's the CO of this unit?** Kaidlen asked. Lieutenant Na'tor stepped forward at her question.

**"I am Ma'am. Lieutenant Na'tor. Awaiting your orders Ma'am."** He responded.

**"Thank you Lieutenant. Send out two of your men as point, and to scout. Be wary of any Royal Guards you encounter. We need to try and capture one alive if at all possible. However our first priority is to free Minister Benson, and those in with him. Be ready for any traps, they very well could be prepared for someone trying to make a rescue attempt. We'll need to move fast on this, time is off the essence."** Shan started issuing orders. **"Also designate two troopers to cover our backs. We don't need to be ambushed from behind. Lieutenant, you'll be with me and Neehmai. Have the rest of your men flanking us to either side, only somewhat spread out. When the points reach a corridor, they need to stop and contact the rest of us, so we can double check to make sure that no one is waiting to ambush from the sides either."**

**"Yes Ma'am."** The Lieutenant responded in agreement. He then issued the orders and the group prepared to head out. Once the orders were issued, every moved out rapidly.

Taking off at a fast jog, Kaidlen moved rapidly through the corridor. Little resistance was encountered as the group moved rapidly. However, once they reached the area where the Minister was being held captive, the Colonel and her troops encountered a sizable group.

**"It's going to be a battle, Lieutenant. I will do my best to deflect incoming blaster bolts away from you and your men."**" Kaidlen stated, as the Colonal ignited her lightsaber again. With a primal cry, she leapt forward as the troopers opened fire on the traitors.

**"Give the Colonel some cover, men. Fire when ready."** Na'tor ordered, as he started aiming at targets himself.

Kaidlen moved like a dancer, as she moved forward towards the enemy. Her feet were silent on the floor, as she weaved between and batted away incoming fire. One graceful foot stretched outwards and up, connecting with the chin of one guard and snapping his head back. The guard's head impacted against the wall with a large thunk, then his body slithered to the floor.

The battle was intense, the former Royal Guards not wanting to give way before the catlike woman. Those not picked off by the troopers died quickly under the barely visible blade of a lightsaber. The miasma of combat filled the area, making it harder to pick off the enemy. Then the blasts died down, and the smoke started to clear.

Colonel Shan pounded on the sealed door.

**"Minister Benson, it's Lieutenant Colonel Kaidlen Shan. You and your people are safe now. You can safely open the door, once you are able to do so."** She informed the people inside the room.

When the door opened, Minister Benson and his staff would see a figure that looked like a warrior goddess of some primitive Outer Rim planent. Kaidlen was covered with dirt and streaked with soot and blood. Her striped skin was obvious, since her bare legs stuck out beneath the armored chest piece that she wore over shorty pjs. A silver handled lightsaber with it's nearly invisible blade gleamed in her hand, as she softly panted from her exertions.

**Praetor-General Kaidlen Shan**

Commandant of the Royal Guard

Imperial Republic High Councilor

## Post 169 by Jaylen Myst on 2008-12-03T19:33:38Z



Jaylen gasped with suprise as Koru flung himself onto Jaylen. He also involuntarily jumped when a large explosion resounded into the room with dangerously close proximity. The force of the blast flung Jaylen, Koru, and the table back into the wall where Jaylen was met with blackness.

Jaylen groaned as he regained conciousness. He pushed something heavy off of him and stood up leaning against the wall for support. He shook his head clearing the fog inside his head and suddenly felt his whole body ache. He gave himself a quick look over: just cuts and bruises and ringing of the ears from the...explosion?! He grunted and coughed again as everything that had happened in the past hour rushed back to him. He jerked his head around the room searching for Koru. The doorway and room itself were in shambles. The walls were intact and jet black where the explosion had occured. The various things that were in the room were either completely destroyed or beyond repair. Everything else was rubble. He closed his eyes and shook his head again. He then made things easeir as he detected an unconsious mind. Jaylen looked down and saw that Koru was that "heavy something". As with himself, Koru looked as if he had mainly cuts and bruises. He picked up his staff was next to the unconsious Koru and debated whether to get help or not. He decided that he would stay put in case any enemy soldiers that were still around decided to pay a visit.

As he waited for Koru to regain consiousness, he realized that he had no idea what had happen or what had caused that explosion. All he knew was that Koru had thrown himself onto him to protect him from the blast.

*Why was I such a coward? I don't understand. One minute I have some confidence and the next I'm frozen with fear. Why?! I should have been helping him. We could have easily fought them off! What's wrong with...*Jaylen was interupted from his thoughts as Koru begin to stir.

## Post 170 by Koru Tav on 2008-12-03T22:06:29Z



Koru groaned as he regained consciousness. His eyes were still closed and he realized that his whole body was in pain from the aftermath. *At least I know I’m alive.* Probably from bruises and scrapes Koru guessed. Koru also realized that his ears were ringing.  *Note to self: activating a det pack in close proximity is sometimes not the smartest thing in the world.* Koru opened his eyes, shook his head to clear his head, then got up. He gritted his teeth from a sharp pain in his side. He knew it was a rib or two and he also knew for a fact that it wasn’t broken. He had a broken rib in the past. He was in pain…but not THAT much pain. It decided that it was just bruised.

**As he finished getting up, Jaylen started talking to him I’m sorry, I don’t know what came over me. I don’t know why I was so scared. Forgive me please!**

Koru silenced him by holding his hand up. He took a deep breathe, doing his best to mask the pain, and then spoke, “ **I don’t what came over you either, but…both of us are alive and in good shape**  Koru paused to reword what he had just said **better shape than the guys that were out there.”** Koru gestured his head towards the doorway while emphasizing “were”. Koru looked directly at Jaylen and tried to cheer him up although he still had som e doubt about him in the back of his mind  **Get over it. Everyone gets scared in combat sometimes. Let get going.**

As Koru started to walk there was a sharp pain in his leg. *Just what I need an injured leg as well as a rib. Oh well, nothing like a little pain to keep you awake.*  He managed to retrieve his bow and several arrows that had fallen out of his quiver and then motioned for Jaylen to follow. With a limp he made to the doorway then stopped with surprise as Jaylen came to his left side and took his arm (Koru) and draped it around his (Jaylen) shoulders.

**You don’t need to that** Koru said his pride getting in the way. Granted, Koru liked helping others, but when it came to people helping him, it was a different story.

**You’re right I don’t** Jaylen retorted **but your leg is hurt.**

**I’m fine.**

**You mask it well, but I’m not stupid.** The two argued somewhat for the next few seconds and Jaylen didn’t budge.

**Fine, you win. But I drive.** Koru said in a matter of speaking then relinquished his arm to Jaylen’s shoulders and they headed in the genereal direction of where he had left the prince fighting Kennedy. **You’re stubborn, you know that.**

Jaylen nodded absentmindedly. A long thirty seconds later Jaylen broke the silence with a tinge of surprise **So you’re not mad at me?**

**No. Why would I be?**

**I didn’t help you back there…and I didn’t follow what you told me to do.**

**Jaylen** Koru sighed as he stopped, looked at staff on Jaylen’s back, then looked at him directly **Like I said back there, get over it. Everyone get scared while they’re fighting. Besides, I trust you enough. You could’ve killed me numerous times in the past hour. Granted I’m still curious about what happen back there, but I believe you when you say you don’t know exactly what happen. And if you think you’re in trouble…get over that too! You’re not. Whatever would happen to you before won't happen now.** The Lt. looked forward let keep moving. They exchange a few more words along the way until they reached the scene of the finished duel.

## Post 172 by James Stratus on 2008-12-06T08:10:11Z



The High Prince suddenly became concious. He felt a cold hard metalic floor underneath him, and his head hurt immensely. He must have hit the ground hard. *I may have suffered a concussion, or who knows what else. I hope its not too bad. Hopefully the healing trance helped it some.* He noticed large metal objects surrounding him completely everywhere he could see, and at every angle. As he was able to look closer and in focus, he was able to make more sense out of what he was seeing. He was seeing the backs of the Sentinel-class battle droids that were protecting him. He could hear the hum of each of their personal shields encompassing him and themselves in a reinforced shield grid. *These guys are more impressive than I thought.* He began to get up. **"Commander, get a medical and emergency maintance team down here right away. Contact the IRSB and have them send an investigation team down here to the scene to see if they find anything useful, like on the bodies of our enemies or perhaps any still alive. Make sure the wounded are treated first. Relay those orders immediately through your comlink. That is, if communications are restored."**

**"Yes my lord."** came the deep and commanding metallic voice of the droid commander. Immediately signals began to be transmitted to the Palace Situation Room where the messages would be relayed to the proper authorities. Alpha Point was about to get crowded fast. The Prince had no intention of staying. He needed to get to Nexus Core to make sure there was no damage, and to make sure that everything was done right. **"I am going to be heading to Nexus Core. I believe that is in your databanks at the Ubiqtorate Sanctuary for you to access information on by my voice command authorization active as of now. I want you to send a few of your Sentinels to search out the area for any survivors, especially among those who accompanied me. If you locate Lieutenant Koru Tav, inform him that he is to first see that he is inspected and treated by medical personnel and then he will meet me at the Situation Room. I will be there after I visit the Core. Leave a team to secure Alpha Point, the rest come with me."**

**"My lord, we will obey. I am not programmed to question orders, but I have been observing my lord's physical ailments during the course of this situation and I have observed that my lord needs medical attention and that it could be serious. I submit to my lord my reccommendation to consider seeking medical treatment immediately as part of my protective programming."**

The High Prince smiled and shook his head. **"You haven't encountered many Jedi before, have you Commander?"**

**"Negative my lord. However I do not see how that makes much of a diff...."**

The High Prince cut him off with a wave of his hand. **"No, you are partially correct, Commander. I will seek medical attention after I visit Nexus Core. I must go there. If anything should happen to me, Commander, allow my friends to see that I recieve proper treatment, and do not interfere."**

The battle droid commander made as if to nod, but said nothing and fell into formation. The other droids reacted immediately and fell in to guard formation of the Supreme Ruler. The trip to Nexus Core was rather quick from Alpha Point. There was a direct route, as Lord Kennedy had discovered, or previously known about. The High Prince had told no one of this, not even Lord Kennedy, or Layha. He pressed forward, leading the way. It was not on any files, it was a direct tunnel, with no security systems other than alarms that triggered and alerted himself via comlink, which obviously had been disabled somehow. He overheard two familiar voices talking. He was beginning to feel a little dizzy.

**"With Communications now restored, we should be able to contact the High Prince."** he heard Admiral OVERMIND say.

**"Don't bother,"** a gravely voice said. It was the voice of the High Prince, and the clanking of metallic footsteps of the battledroid regiment accompanying him could be heard as they approached escorting the High Prince. They were a very impressive site for the eyes, scary perhaps, to some. **"I'm right here,"** the Prince responded. With that, he looked at them both, and began to feel weak again. He felt he was going to pass out, but he would be alright. He just needed to rest and allow his body to recover from the great ordeal that had taken place. **"Well done, my friends."** he said to them both, identifying the individuals to the droids as friends. And then he collapsed right there on the ground in front of them both.

## Post 177 by Khyron Overmind on 2008-12-25T15:48:23Z



The Admiral turned around to the arriving colonel. He was tired and had not sensed her arrival until the last moment. However, he waited until she talked to turn around, since he knew his back was covered.

**"Colonel, I am glad to see you. As you can see, we know have the situation under control. We are now waiting for the arrival of the medical unit in order to move the High Prince to a different location. It is just a precaution, since he is not heart, just needs tons and tons of rest."**

## Post 178 by Kaidlen Shan on 2008-12-28T22:44:41Z



Kaidlen almost jumped out of her skin when Admiral OVERMIND all but appeared out of thin air. She had not sensed him at all, her attention focused on the High Prince.

**"Thank you Admiral. I have managed to rescue Minister Benson and his staff. They should be relocating to a more secure location now."** The Colonel reported, tossing a salute towards the Minister of Defence. **"I hope that some of that incoming medical aid is going to be for your benefit Sir. You can use it yourself."**

Kaidlen knew that she had no room to talk on that score. She would seek additional treatment in due time.

## Post 181 by Khyron Overmind on 2009-01-03T22:28:56Z



Soon enough the medical team finally arrived and, with them, several additional soldiers to serve as reinforcements to protect the High Prince. The medics checked on the Prince and issued the same diagnostic that had been previously mentioned: he needed rest, lots of it. With that established, they put him into a portable sled and started to move him toward another wing of the Palace, one more suited to serve as a recovery room.

As they started to move, the Admiral's datapad started to beep with several incoming messages, which meant that the communications had been restored and this crisis would be coming to an end. The Admiral ignored yet the outcome of the battles in the different parts of Coruscant, both in orbit and on the ground, but he got a feeling that they had succeeded.... At least for now.
